=== FILE: backend/src/app.js ===
const fastify = require('fastify')({
  logger: true
});

const cors = require('@fastify/cors');
const jwt = require('@fastify/jwt');
require('dotenv').config();

// 1. Configurazione CORS
fastify.register(cors, {
  origin: '*',
  methods: ['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'DELETE', 'OPTIONS']
});

// 2. Configurazione JWT
fastify.register(jwt, {
  secret: process.env.JWT_SECRET || 'super_secret_jwt_key_maglite_2026'
});

// 3. Decoratore per proteggere le rotte
// Qualsiasi rotta che usa preValidation: [fastify.authenticate] verificherà il token
fastify.decorate('authenticate', async function (request, reply) {
  try {
    if (!request.headers.authorization && request.query.token) {
      request.headers.authorization = `Bearer ${request.query.token}`;
    }
    await request.jwtVerify();
  } catch (err) {
    reply.code(401).send({ error: 'Non autorizzato. Token mancante o non valido.' });
  }
});

// 4. Registrazione Rotte
fastify.register(require('./routes/auth'), { prefix: '/api/auth' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/pallets'), { prefix: '/api/pallets' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/inbound'), { prefix: '/api/inbound' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/users'), { prefix: '/api/users' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/customers'), { prefix: '/api/customers' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/products'), { prefix: '/api/products' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/locations'), { prefix: '/api/locations' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/dashboard'), { prefix: '/api/dashboard' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/audit'), { prefix: '/api/audit' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/outbound'), { prefix: '/api/outbound' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/billing'), { prefix: '/api/billing' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/settings'), { prefix: '/api/settings' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/finances'), { prefix: '/api/finances' });
fastify.register(require('./routes/tms'), { prefix: '/api/tms' });

// Health check endpoint (non protetto)
fastify.get('/api/health', async () => {
  return { status: 'ok', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };
});

// 5. Avvio del server
const start = async () => {
  try {
    const port = process.env.PORT || 3000;
    const host = process.env.HOST || '0.0.0.0';
    
    // Inizializza Socket.io
    const io = require('socket.io')(fastify.server, {
      cors: { origin: '*', methods: ['GET', 'POST', 'OPTIONS', 'PUT', 'DELETE'] }
    });
    fastify.decorate('io', io);
    
    io.on('connection', (socket) => {
      fastify.log.info(`Socket Client Connected: ${socket.id}`);
    });

    // Run Migrations
    const runMigrations = require('./scripts/migrate');
    await runMigrations();

    await fastify.ready();
    await fastify.listen({ port, host });
    fastify.log.info(`🚀 MagLite Backend in ascolto su ${host}:${port}`);
  } catch (err) {
    fastify.log.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
  }
};

start();

const mysql = require('mysql2/promise');
require('dotenv').config();

// Configurazione del Pool di connessioni per MySQL
const pool = mysql.createPool({
  host: process.env.DB_HOST || '127.0.0.1',
  port: process.env.DB_PORT || 3306,
  user: process.env.DB_USER || 'maglite_user',
  password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD || 'maglite_pass',
  database: process.env.DB_NAME || 'maglite_db',
  timezone: '+00:00', // FIXED: Ensures Node parses DB time correctly as UTC instead of Local
  waitForConnections: true,
  connectionLimit: 10,
  queueLimit: 0
});

// Test della connessione all'avvio
pool.getConnection()
  .then(conn => {
    console.log('✅ Connesso al database MySQL con successo!');
    conn.release();
  })
  .catch(err => {
    console.error('❌ Errore critico: impossibile connettersi a MySQL:', err.message);
  });

module.exports = pool;
=== FILE: backend/src/routes/audit.js ===
const db = require('../config/db');

async function auditRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  fastify.get('/', async (request, reply) => {
    const { search = '', start_date, end_date } = request.query;

    try {
      let query = 'SELECT * FROM AUDIT_LOGS WHERE 1=1';
      const params = [];

      if (search) {
        query += ' AND (details LIKE ? OR action LIKE ? OR username LIKE ?)';
        params.push(`%${search}%`, `%${search}%`, `%${search}%`);
      }

      if (start_date) {
        query += ' AND DATE(created_at) >= ?';
        params.push(start_date);
      }

      if (end_date) {
        query += ' AND DATE(created_at) <= ?';
        params.push(end_date);
      }

      query += ' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 500';

      const [logs] = await db.query(query, params);
      return logs;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore recupero audit logs' });
    }
  });
}

module.exports = auditRoutes;
=== FILE: backend/src/routes/auth.js ===
const bcrypt = require('bcrypt');
const db = require('../config/db');

async function authRoutes(fastify, options) {
  
  // Endpoint di Login (Pubblico)
  fastify.post('/login', async (request, reply) => {
    const { username, password } = request.body;
    
    if (!username || !password) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Username e password sono obbligatori' });
    }

    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM USERS WHERE username = ?', [username]);
      const user = rows[0];

      if (!user) {
        return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'Credenziali non valide' });
      }

      const match = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password_hash);
      if (!match) {
        return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'Credenziali non valide' });
      }

      // Genera il token JWT includendo ruolo e ID (Senza scadenza)
      const token = fastify.jwt.sign({ 
        id: user.id, 
        username: user.username, 
        role: user.role,
        requires_password_change: !!user.requires_password_change
      });

      return {
        token,
        user: {
          id: user.id,
          username: user.username,
          role: user.role,
          requires_password_change: !!user.requires_password_change,
          preferences: user.preferences ? (typeof user.preferences === 'string' ? JSON.parse(user.preferences) : user.preferences) : null
        }
      };

    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore interno del server' });
    }
  });

  // Endpoint di Supporto: Setup Admin iniziale (Pubblico per MVP)
  // Utilizzato per creare il primissimo utente admin per poter testare il login
  fastify.post('/setup-admin', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [[{ count }]] = await db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM USERS');
      if (count > 0) return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Setup già completato' });

      const { username, password } = request.body || {};
      
      if (!username || !password) {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Username e password obbligatori' });
      }

      // Cifra la password prima di salvarla nel DB
      const hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10);
      await db.query('INSERT INTO USERS (username, password_hash, role, requires_password_change) VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE)', [username, hash, 'developer']);
      
      return { success: true, message: `Utente developer '${username}' creato con successo!` };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Utente già esistente' });
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore interno del server' });
    }
  });

  // Endpoint per l'utente che deve cambiare la propria password (Protetto)
  fastify.post('/change-password', { preValidation: [fastify.authenticate] }, async (request, reply) => {
    const { newPassword } = request.body;
    
    if (!newPassword || newPassword.length < 6) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'La nuova password deve contenere almeno 6 caratteri' });
    }

    try {
      const hash = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, 10);
      
      await db.query('UPDATE USERS SET password_hash = ?, requires_password_change = FALSE WHERE id = ?', [hash, request.user.id]);
      
      // Rigeneriamo il token per rimuovere il flag requires_password_change (Senza scadenza)
      const newToken = fastify.jwt.sign({ 
        id: request.user.id, 
        username: request.user.username, 
        role: request.user.role,
        requires_password_change: false
      });

      return { success: true, message: 'Password aggiornata con successo', token: newToken };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore interno del server' });
    }
  });

  fastify.put('/preferences', { preValidation: [fastify.authenticate] }, async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const preferences = JSON.stringify(request.body.preferences);
      await db.query('UPDATE USERS SET preferences = ? WHERE id = ?', [preferences, request.user.id]);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante il salvataggio delle preferenze' });
    }
  });
}

module.exports = authRoutes;
=== FILE: backend/src/routes/billing.js ===
const db = require('../config/db');

async function billingRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  
  // GET Rules
  fastify.get('/rules', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM billing_rules ORDER BY rule_type, rule_code');
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore fetch regole' });
    }
  });

  // POST Rule
  fastify.post('/rules', async (request, reply) => {
    const { rule_code, description, default_price, rule_type } = request.body;
    try {
      const [result] = await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO billing_rules (rule_code, description, default_price, rule_type) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        [rule_code, description, default_price || 0, rule_type]
      );
      return { success: true, id: result.insertId };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore creazione regola, forse il codice esiste già?' });
    }
  });

  // PUT Rule
  fastify.put('/rules/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    const { description, default_price, rule_type } = request.body;
    try {
      await db.query(
        'UPDATE billing_rules SET description = ?, default_price = ?, rule_type = ? WHERE id = ?',
        [description, default_price, rule_type, request.params.id]
      );
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore modifica regola' });
    }
  });

  // DELETE Rule
  fastify.delete('/rules/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      await db.query('DELETE FROM billing_rules WHERE id = ?', [request.params.id]);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore eliminazione regola (forse in uso da client_tariffs?)' });
    }
  });


  // GET Tariffs
  fastify.get('/tariffs/:clientId', async (request, reply) => {
    const { clientId } = request.params;
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT 
          br.rule_code, 
          br.description, 
          br.default_price, 
          br.rule_type,
          ct.custom_price 
        FROM billing_rules br
        LEFT JOIN client_tariffs ct ON br.rule_code = ct.rule_code AND ct.customer_id = ?
        ORDER BY br.rule_type, br.rule_code
      `, [clientId]);
      return rows.map(r => ({
        ...r,
        final_price: r.custom_price !== null ? parseFloat(r.custom_price) : parseFloat(r.default_price)
      }));
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore fetch tariffe' });
    }
  });

  // POST Tariffs (Upsert)
  fastify.post('/tariffs/:clientId', async (request, reply) => {
    const { clientId } = request.params;
    const { tariffs } = request.body; // [{ rule_code, custom_price }]
    
    if (!tariffs || !Array.isArray(tariffs)) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Formato tariffe non valido' });
    }

    const conn = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await conn.beginTransaction();
      for (const t of tariffs) {
        await conn.query(`
          INSERT INTO client_tariffs (customer_id, rule_code, custom_price) 
          VALUES (?, ?, ?)
          ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE custom_price = ?
        `, [clientId, t.rule_code, t.custom_price, t.custom_price]);
      }
      await conn.commit();
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      await conn.rollback();
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore salvataggio tariffe' });
    } finally {
      conn.release();
    }
  });

  // POST Calculate
  fastify.post('/calculate', async (request, reply) => {
    const { clientId, month, year, includeHistorical = true } = request.body;
    
    if (!clientId || !month || !year) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Client, mese e anno richiesti' });
    }

    try {
      // Carico listino finale del cliente
      const [tariffRows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT br.rule_code, br.description, br.rule_type, COALESCE(ct.custom_price, br.default_price) as price
        FROM billing_rules br
        LEFT JOIN client_tariffs ct ON br.rule_code = ct.rule_code AND ct.customer_id = ?
      `, [clientId]);
      
      const tariffs = {};
      tariffRows.forEach(t => {
        tariffs[t.rule_code] = { price: parseFloat(t.price), desc: t.description };
      });

      // Calcolo date del mese selezionato
      const startDate = new Date(year, month - 1, 1);
      const endDate = new Date(year, month, 0, 23, 59, 59);

      // Trovo le palette entrate, uscite e giacenti
      // IN: created_at nel mese.
      // OUT: shipped_at nel mese.
      // STORAGE: created_at <= endDate AND (shipped_at IS NULL OR shipped_at >= startDate)
      
      const [pallets] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.pallet_code, p.created_at,
               COALESCE(p.shipped_at, IF(p.status = 'SHIPPED', p.created_at, NULL)) as shipped_at,
               p.flags, p.quantity, p.pallet_uom, p.batch, pr.name as product_name
        FROM PALLETS p
        LEFT JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
        WHERE p.customer_id = ? 
        AND p.created_at <= ? 
        AND (
          COALESCE(p.shipped_at, IF(p.status = 'SHIPPED', p.created_at, NULL)) IS NULL
          OR COALESCE(p.shipped_at, IF(p.status = 'SHIPPED', p.created_at, NULL)) >= ?
        )
      `, [clientId, endDate, startDate]);

      const items = [];
      const palletDetails = [];
      let total = 0;

      const addCost = (group, code, desc, qty, priceOverride) => {
        const price = priceOverride !== undefined ? priceOverride : (tariffs[code]?.price || 0);
        if (price === 0) return;
        const existing = items.find(i => i.code === code && i.price === price);
        if (existing) {
          existing.qty += qty;
          existing.subtotal += (qty * price);
        } else {
          items.push({ id: Math.random().toString(36).substring(7), group, code, description: desc || tariffs[code]?.desc || code, qty, price, subtotal: qty * price });
        }
        total += (qty * price);
      };

      // Raggruppo per pallet_code per evitare sovrafatturazione sulle palette miste (più righe con lo stesso pallet_code)
      const groupedPalletsMap = new Map();
      pallets.forEach(p => {
        const code = p.pallet_code;
        if (!groupedPalletsMap.has(code)) {
          groupedPalletsMap.set(code, []);
        }
        groupedPalletsMap.get(code).push(p);
      });

      groupedPalletsMap.forEach((rows, code) => {
        const createdTimes = rows.map(r => new Date(r.created_at).getTime()).filter(t => !isNaN(t));
        const inDate = createdTimes.length > 0 ? new Date(Math.min(...createdTimes)) : new Date(rows[0].created_at);

        const allShipped = rows.every(r => Boolean(r.shipped_at));
        const shippedTimes = allShipped ? rows.map(r => new Date(r.shipped_at).getTime()).filter(t => !isNaN(t)) : [];
        const outDate = (allShipped && shippedTimes.length > 0) ? new Date(Math.max(...shippedTimes)) : null;

        // Unione deduplicata dei flag della paletta
        const uniqueFlagsSet = new Set();
        rows.forEach(r => {
          let parsed = [];
          try {
            parsed = r.flags ? (typeof r.flags === 'string' ? JSON.parse(r.flags) : r.flags) : [];
          } catch (e) {
            parsed = [];
          }
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
            parsed.forEach(f => {
              if (f) uniqueFlagsSet.add(String(f).trim());
            });
          }
        });
        const uniqueFlags = Array.from(uniqueFlagsSet);

        if (rows.length === 1) {
          const p = rows[0];
          palletDetails.push({
            pallet_code: code,
            product_name: p.product_name,
            quantity: p.quantity,
            pallet_uom: p.pallet_uom || 'Pezzi',
            batch: p.batch,
            created_at: inDate,
            shipped_at: outDate,
            flags: uniqueFlags.length > 0 ? JSON.stringify(uniqueFlags) : null
          });
        } else {
          const uniqueProducts = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.product_name).filter(Boolean))];
          const uniqueBatches = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.batch).filter(Boolean))];
          const totalQty = rows.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (parseFloat(r.quantity) || 0), 0);
          const uniqueUoms = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.pallet_uom || 'Pezzi'))];

          palletDetails.push({
            pallet_code: code,
            product_name: uniqueProducts.length > 1
              ? `Paletta Mista (${uniqueProducts.join(', ')})`
              : `${uniqueProducts[0] || 'Articolo'} (${rows.length} lotti)`,
            quantity: totalQty,
            pallet_uom: uniqueUoms.join('/'),
            batch: uniqueBatches.join(', ') || '-',
            created_at: inDate,
            shipped_at: outDate,
            flags: uniqueFlags.length > 0 ? JSON.stringify(uniqueFlags) : null
          });
        }

        // 1. INBOUND (1 solo addebito per pallet_code)
        if (inDate >= startDate && inDate <= endDate) {
          addCost('EVENTI', 'IN_PALLET', tariffs['IN_PALLET']?.desc, 1);
        }
        
        // 2. OUTBOUND (1 solo addebito per pallet_code)
        if (outDate && outDate >= startDate && outDate <= endDate) {
          addCost('EVENTI', 'OUT_PALLET', tariffs['OUT_PALLET']?.desc, 1);
        }

        // 3. STORAGE (Regola Quindicina - 1 solo addebito per pallet_code)
        if (inDate <= endDate && (!outDate || outDate >= startDate)) {
          // Se la paletta è entrata PRIMA di questo mese, si conta per intero.
          if (inDate < startDate) {
            if (includeHistorical) {
              addCost('GIACENZA', 'SOSTA_15', tariffs['SOSTA_15']?.desc, 1);
            }
          } else {
            // Se è entrata in QUESTO mese
            if (inDate.getDate() <= 15) {
              addCost('GIACENZA', 'SOSTA_15', tariffs['SOSTA_15']?.desc, 1);
            } else {
              addCost('GIACENZA', 'SOSTA_MAGGIORE_15', tariffs['SOSTA_MAGGIORE_15']?.desc, 1);
            }
          }
        }

        // 4. FLAGS (EXTRA - 1 solo addebito per flag unico su ogni pallet_code)
        uniqueFlags.forEach(f => {
          const ruleCode = `FLAG_${f.toUpperCase()}`;
          // Usa il prezzo da tariffe se esiste
          if (tariffs[ruleCode]) {
            // I flag vengono addebitati solo per le palette a giacenza o eventi in quel mese
            // Se la paletta è movimentata o stoccata, paga l'extra.
            addCost('SERVIZI EXTRA', ruleCode, tariffs[ruleCode].desc, 1);
          }
        });
      });

      // Raggruppo per risposta
      
      // Aggiungo anche eventuali sconti automatici se presenti in client_tariffs
      // ma il calcolo di sconti lo farà dinamicamente il Frontend con le regole manuali e sconti selezionati dall'utente.
      
      return {
        clientId,
        period: `${month.toString().padStart(2, '0')}/${year}`,
        items: items.sort((a, b) => a.group.localeCompare(b.group)),
        palletDetails,
        total
      };

    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante il calcolo' });
    }
  });
}

module.exports = billingRoutes;
=== FILE: backend/src/routes/customers.js ===
const db = require('../config/db');

async function customerRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  fastify.addHook('preHandler', async (request, reply) => {
    if (request.user.role !== 'developer' && request.user.role !== 'backoffice') {
      return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Accesso negato.' });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/customers
  fastify.get('/', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM CUSTOMERS ORDER BY business_name ASC');
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante il recupero dei clienti' });
    }
  });

  // POST /api/customers
  fastify.post('/', async (request, reply) => {
    const { unique_id, business_name, vat_number, address, phone, email } = request.body;
    
    if (!unique_id || !business_name) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Codice Cliente e Ragione Sociale sono obbligatori' });
    }

    try {
      const [result] = await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO CUSTOMERS (unique_id, business_name, vat_number, address, phone, email) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
        [unique_id, business_name, vat_number, address, phone, email]
      );
      
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['CREATE_CUSTOMER', `Creato cliente ${business_name} (${unique_id})`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );

      return reply.code(201).send({ success: true, id: result.insertId });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Codice Cliente già in uso' });
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore interno' });
    }
  });

  // DELETE /api/customers/:id
  fastify.delete('/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    const { id } = request.params;
    try {
      const [[customer]] = await db.query('SELECT business_name FROM CUSTOMERS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      const [result] = await db.query('DELETE FROM CUSTOMERS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      if (result.affectedRows === 0) {
        return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Cliente non trovato' });
      }
      
      if (customer) {
        await db.query(
          'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
          ['DELETE_CUSTOMER', `Eliminato cliente ${customer.business_name} (ID: ${id})`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
        );
      }
      
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Impossibile eliminare (potrebbe avere movimenti o referenze associate)' });
    }
  });
  // PUT /api/customers/:id
  fastify.put('/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    const { id } = request.params;
    const { unique_id, business_name, vat_number, address, phone, email } = request.body;
    
    if (!unique_id || !business_name) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Codice Cliente e Ragione Sociale sono obbligatori' });
    }

    try {
      const [result] = await db.query(
        'UPDATE CUSTOMERS SET unique_id = ?, business_name = ?, vat_number = ?, address = ?, phone = ?, email = ? WHERE id = ?',
        [unique_id, business_name, vat_number, address, phone, email, id]
      );
      if (result.affectedRows === 0) {
        return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Cliente non trovato' });
      }

      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['UPDATE_CUSTOMER', `Modificato cliente ${business_name} (${unique_id})`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );

      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Codice Cliente già in uso' });
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore interno' });
    }
  });
}

module.exports = customerRoutes;
=== FILE: backend/src/routes/dashboard.js ===
const db = require('../config/db');

async function dashboardRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  fastify.get('/stats', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      // Calcolo Saturazione Posizioni
      const [[{ total }]] = await db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM LOCATIONS');
      const [[{ occupied }]] = await db.query('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT location) as occupied FROM PALLETS WHERE status = "STOCKED" AND location IS NOT NULL AND location != ""');
      
      const saturation_percentage = total > 0 ? Math.round((occupied / total) * 100) : 0;

      // Ultimi Logs
      const [recent_logs] = await db.query(`
        SELECT id, action, details, source, username, created_at
        FROM AUDIT_LOGS
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
        LIMIT 10
      `);

      for (let log of recent_logs) {
        const match = log.details.match(/paletta (PAL-[A-Z0-9\-]+)/i);
        if (match) {
          log.pallet_code = match[1];
          const [[p]] = await db.query(`
            SELECT pr.name as product_name
            FROM PALLETS p JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
            WHERE p.pallet_code = ?
          `, [log.pallet_code]);
          if (p) {
            log.product_name = p.product_name;
          }
        }
      }

      return {
        total_locations: total,
        occupied_locations: occupied,
        saturation_percentage,
        recent_logs
      };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore recupero statistiche dashboard' });
    }
  });
}

const db = require('../config/db');

async function financesRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  // --- INVOICES (PROFORME SALVATE) ---
  
  fastify.post('/invoices', async (request, reply) => {
    const { customer_id, period_month, period_year, base_total, vat_amount, grand_total, invoice_data } = request.body;
    try {
      await db.query(`
        INSERT INTO invoices (customer_id, period_month, period_year, base_total, vat_amount, grand_total, invoice_data)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE 
          base_total = VALUES(base_total),
          vat_amount = VALUES(vat_amount),
          grand_total = VALUES(grand_total),
          invoice_data = VALUES(invoice_data)
      `, [customer_id, period_month, period_year, base_total, vat_amount, grand_total, invoice_data ? JSON.stringify(invoice_data) : null]);
      
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['SAVE_INVOICE', `Salvata proforma per cliente ID: ${customer_id}, Periodo: ${period_month}/${period_year}`, 'Gestionale Finanza', request.user.username]
      );

      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore salvataggio fattura' });
    }
  });

  fastify.get('/invoices', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT i.*, c.business_name, c.address, c.vat_number, c.unique_id
        FROM invoices i
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON i.customer_id = c.id
        ORDER BY i.period_year DESC, i.period_month DESC, i.created_at DESC
      `);
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore fetch fatture' });
    }
  });

  fastify.delete('/invoices/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      await db.query('DELETE FROM invoices WHERE id = ?', [request.params.id]);
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['DELETE_INVOICE', `Eliminata proforma ID: ${request.params.id}`, 'Gestionale Finanza', request.user.username]
      );
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore eliminazione proforma' });
    }
  });

  // --- EXPENSES (USCITE) ---

  
  fastify.put('/invoices/:id/payment', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT is_paid FROM invoices WHERE id = ?', [request.params.id]);
      if (rows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Fattura non trovata' });
      
      const newStatus = rows[0].is_paid ? 0 : 1;
      await db.query('UPDATE invoices SET is_paid = ? WHERE id = ?', [newStatus, request.params.id]);
      
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['TOGGLE_PAYMENT', `Impostato stato ${newStatus ? 'PAGATO' : 'DA PAGARE'} su proforma ID: ${request.params.id}`, 'Gestionale Finanza', request.user.username]
      );
      
      return { success: true, is_paid: newStatus };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore aggiornamento stato pagamento' });
    }
  });

  fastify.get('/expenses', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM expenses ORDER BY expense_date DESC');
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore fetch uscite' });
    }
  });

  fastify.post('/expenses', async (request, reply) => {
    const { description, amount, expense_date, category } = request.body;
    try {
      await db.query(`
        INSERT INTO expenses (description, amount, expense_date, category)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
      `, [description, amount, expense_date, category || 'Generale']);
      
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['CREATE_EXPENSE', `Registrata spesa: ${description} di EUR ${amount}`, 'Gestionale Finanza', request.user.username]
      );

      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore salvataggio uscita' });
    }
  });

  fastify.delete('/expenses/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      await db.query('DELETE FROM expenses WHERE id = ?', [request.params.id]);
      
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['DELETE_EXPENSE', `Eliminata spesa ID: ${request.params.id}`, 'Gestionale Finanza', request.user.username]
      );

      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore eliminazione uscita' });
    }
  });

  // --- ANALYTICS & FORECAST ---

  fastify.get('/analytics', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [incomes] = await db.query(`
        SELECT period_year as year, period_month as month, 
               SUM(CASE WHEN is_paid = TRUE THEN base_total ELSE 0 END) as total_income,
               SUM(CASE WHEN is_paid = FALSE THEN base_total ELSE 0 END) as unpaid_income
        FROM invoices
        GROUP BY period_year, period_month
        ORDER BY period_year ASC, period_month ASC
      `);

      const [expenses] = await db.query(`
        SELECT YEAR(expense_date) as year, MONTH(expense_date) as month, SUM(amount) as total_expense
        FROM expenses
        GROUP BY YEAR(expense_date), MONTH(expense_date)
        ORDER BY year ASC, month ASC
      `);

      const timelineMap = {};
      
      incomes.forEach(i => {
        const key = `${i.year}-${i.month.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
        timelineMap[key] = { monthLabel: key, income: parseFloat(i.total_income), unpaid: parseFloat(i.unpaid_income), expense: 0, profit: parseFloat(i.total_income) };
      });

      expenses.forEach(e => {
        const key = `${e.year}-${e.month.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
        if (!timelineMap[key]) {
          timelineMap[key] = { monthLabel: key, income: 0, unpaid: 0, expense: parseFloat(e.total_expense), profit: -parseFloat(e.total_expense) };
        } else {
          timelineMap[key].expense = parseFloat(e.total_expense);
          timelineMap[key].profit = timelineMap[key].income - timelineMap[key].expense;
        }
      });

      const timeline = Object.values(timelineMap).sort((a, b) => a.monthLabel.localeCompare(b.monthLabel));

      const [invoices] = await db.query(`
        SELECT i.customer_id, c.business_name, i.period_year, i.period_month, i.base_total
        FROM invoices i
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON i.customer_id = c.id
        ORDER BY i.customer_id, i.period_year DESC, i.period_month DESC
      `);

      const customersMap = {};
      invoices.forEach(inv => {
        if (!customersMap[inv.customer_id]) {
          customersMap[inv.customer_id] = {
            id: inv.customer_id,
            name: inv.business_name,
            history: []
          };
        }
        customersMap[inv.customer_id].history.push(parseFloat(inv.base_total));
      });

      const customerForecasts = Object.values(customersMap).map(c => {
        const h = c.history;
        let lastMonth = h.length > 0 ? h[0] : 0;
        let prevMonth = h.length > 1 ? h[1] : 0;
        
        let forecast = 0;
        let trend = 0;
        if (prevMonth > 0) {
          trend = ((lastMonth - prevMonth) / prevMonth) * 100;
        } else if (lastMonth > 0) {
          trend = 100;
        }

        if (h.length >= 3) {
          forecast = ((h[0] * 3) + (h[1] * 2) + (h[2] * 1)) / 6;
        } else if (h.length === 2) {
          forecast = ((h[0] * 2) + (h[1] * 1)) / 3;
        } else if (h.length === 1) {
          forecast = h[0];
        }

        return {
          id: c.id,
          name: c.name,
          lastMonth,
          prevMonth,
          trend: trend.toFixed(2),
          forecast: forecast.toFixed(2)
        };
      }).sort((a, b) => b.lastMonth - a.lastMonth);
      
      return { timeline, customerForecasts };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore caricamento statistiche' });
    }
  });

}

module.exports = financesRoutes;
=== FILE: backend/src/routes/inbound.js ===
const db = require('../config/db');
const { drawLabel, generateLabelsPdfBuffer, sanitizeExpirationDate } = require('../utils/pdfLabels');

async function inboundRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  // 1. Create Inbound DDT
  fastify.post('/ddt', async (request, reply) => {
    const { cart } = request.body;
    if (!cart || cart.length === 0) return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Carrello vuoto' });

    const connection = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();

      const customer_id = cart[0].customer_id;
      
      // Generate DDT Number
      const [rows] = await connection.query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM INBOUND_DDTS WHERE YEAR(created_at) = YEAR(CURRENT_DATE)');
      const nextId = rows[0].count + 1;
      const ddtNumber = `IN-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${nextId.toString().padStart(4, '0')}`;

      const [resDdt] = await connection.query(
        'INSERT INTO INBOUND_DDTS (ddt_number, customer_id, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
        [ddtNumber, customer_id, 'PENDING']
      );
      const ddtId = resDdt.insertId;

      for (const item of cart) {
        const [[prodRow]] = await connection.query('SELECT sku, name FROM PRODUCTS WHERE id = ?', [item.product_id]);
        const itemSku = item.sku || item.client_article_number || prodRow?.sku || null;
        const itemName = prodRow?.name || item.product_name || item.name;
        const itemLocation = item.location ? String(item.location).trim().toUpperCase() : null;
        await connection.query(
          'INSERT INTO INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS (inbound_ddt_id, product_id, sku, product_name, product_uom, expected_quantity, batch, units_per_box, client_pallet_number, client_article_number, expiration_date, pallet_uom, location) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
          [ddtId, item.product_id, itemSku, itemName, item.uom || item.product_uom || 'Pezzi', item.quantity, item.batch, item.units_per_box, item.client_pallet_number, item.client_article_number, sanitizeExpirationDate(item.expiration_date), item.pallet_uom || item.uom || 'Pezzi', itemLocation]
        );
      }

      await connection.commit();
      return { success: true, ddt_id: ddtId, ddt_number: ddtNumber };
    } catch (err) {
      await connection.rollback();
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore creazione DDT' });
    } finally {
      connection.release();
    }
  });

  // 2. Get all Inbound DDTs
  fastify.get('/ddt', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT d.*, c.business_name 
        FROM INBOUND_DDTS d
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON d.customer_id = c.id
        ORDER BY d.created_at DESC
      `);
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore fetch DDTs' });
    }
  });

  // 3. Get Single DDT Details (for Zebra)
  fastify.get('/ddt/:identifier', async (request, reply) => {
    const { identifier } = request.params;
    try {
      const isId = !isNaN(identifier);
      const queryStr = isId ? 'SELECT d.*, c.business_name FROM INBOUND_DDTS d JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON d.customer_id = c.id WHERE d.id = ?' : 'SELECT d.*, c.business_name FROM INBOUND_DDTS d JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON d.customer_id = c.id WHERE d.ddt_number = ?';
      const [ddts] = await db.query(queryStr, [identifier]);
      
      if (ddts.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'DDT non trovato' });
      const ddt = ddts[0];

      const [items] = await db.query(`
        SELECT i.*, 
               COALESCE(NULLIF(i.sku, ''), p.sku) AS sku,
               COALESCE(NULLIF(i.product_name, ''), p.name) AS product_name,
               COALESCE(i.product_uom, p.uom, 'Pezzi') AS product_uom,
               COALESCE(NULLIF(i.units_per_box, 0), p.units_per_box, 1) AS units_per_box
        FROM INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS i
        LEFT JOIN PRODUCTS p ON i.product_id = p.id
        WHERE i.inbound_ddt_id = ?
      `, [ddt.id]);
      
      return { ddt, items };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore fetch DDT details' });
    }
  });

  // 4. Zebra: Generate Pallet for DDT Item
  fastify.post('/ddt/:id/scan', async (request, reply) => {
    const { item_id, quantity, warehouse, location, expiration_date } = request.body;
    const ddt_id = request.params.id;
    const connection = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();

      // Get item
      const [items] = await connection.query('SELECT * FROM INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS WHERE id = ? AND inbound_ddt_id = ?', [item_id, ddt_id]);
      if (items.length === 0) throw new Error('Item non trovato nel DDT');
      const item = items[0];

      const [ddts] = await connection.query('SELECT * FROM INBOUND_DDTS WHERE id = ?', [ddt_id]);
      if (ddts[0].status === 'PENDING') {
        await connection.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDTS SET status = ? WHERE id = ?', ['IN_PROGRESS', ddt_id]);
      }

      // Generate or use existing pallet code based on location
      let palletCode = null;
      let locStr = null;
      if (location) {
        locStr = location.toUpperCase().trim();
        const [existing] = await connection.query(
          "SELECT pallet_code FROM PALLETS WHERE location = ? AND status = 'STOCKED' AND quantity > 0 LIMIT 1",
          [locStr]
        );
        if (existing.length > 0) {
          palletCode = existing[0].pallet_code;
        }
      }
      
      if (!palletCode) {
        const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, '');
        const basePrefix = `PAL-${dateStr}-`;
        const [seqRows] = await connection.query(
          'SELECT DISTINCT pallet_code FROM PALLETS WHERE pallet_code LIKE ?',
          [`${basePrefix}%`]
        );
        let maxSeq = 0;
        for (const r of seqRows) {
          const suffix = String(r.pallet_code || '').slice(basePrefix.length);
          if (/^\d+$/.test(suffix)) {
            const num = parseInt(suffix, 10);
            if (num > maxSeq) maxSeq = num;
          }
        }
        palletCode = `${basePrefix}${String(maxSeq + 1).padStart(4, '0')}`;
      }

      // Insert Pallet
      await connection.query(`
        INSERT INTO PALLETS 
        (pallet_code, customer_id, product_id, quantity, units_per_box, batch, warehouse, status, location, client_pallet_number, client_article_number, expiration_date, pallet_uom, inbound_ddt_id)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `, [
        palletCode, ddts[0].customer_id, item.product_id, quantity, item.units_per_box, item.batch,
        warehouse || 'Settala', locStr ? 'STOCKED' : 'PENDING', locStr || null, item.client_pallet_number, item.client_article_number,
        sanitizeExpirationDate(expiration_date || item.expiration_date), item.pallet_uom, ddt_id
      ]);
      
      await connection.query(
        "INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
        ['INBOUND', `Spunta Zebra: Creata paletta ${palletCode} (${quantity} pz)${locStr ? ' in ' + locStr : ''}`, 'Zebra', request.user ? request.user.username : 'Operatore']
      );

      // Update actual quantity
      await connection.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS SET actual_quantity = actual_quantity + ? WHERE id = ?', [quantity, item_id]);

      await connection.commit();
      return { success: true, pallet_code: palletCode };
    } catch (err) {
      await connection.rollback();
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: err.message || 'Errore scan' });
    } finally {
      connection.release();
    }
  });

  // 5. Complete DDT
  fastify.post('/ddt/:id/complete', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [items] = await db.query('SELECT expected_quantity, actual_quantity FROM INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS WHERE inbound_ddt_id = ?', [request.params.id]);
      let discrepancy = false;
      let totalDiff = 0;
      for (const item of items) {
        if (parseFloat(item.expected_quantity) !== parseFloat(item.actual_quantity)) {
          discrepancy = true;
          totalDiff += (parseFloat(item.actual_quantity) - parseFloat(item.expected_quantity));
        }
      }

      const status = discrepancy ? 'DISCREPANCY' : 'COMPLETED';
      const notes = discrepancy ? `Completato con discrepanza (${totalDiff > 0 ? '+' : ''}${totalDiff})` : null;

      await db.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDTS SET status = ?, closed_at = NOW(), notes = ? WHERE id = ?', [status, notes, request.params.id]);
      
      await db.query('INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)', [
        'COMPLETE_INBOUND_DDT', `Completato DDT ${request.params.id} con stato ${status}`, 'Zebra', request.user.username
      ]);

      return { success: true, status, notes };
    } catch (err) {
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore completamento DDT' });
    }
  });

  // 6. Get DDT PDF
  fastify.get('/ddt/:identifier/pdf', async (request, reply) => {
    const { identifier } = request.params;
    try {
      const PDFDocument = require('pdfkit');
      
      const isId = !isNaN(identifier);
      const queryStr = isId ? 'SELECT d.*, c.business_name FROM INBOUND_DDTS d JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON d.customer_id = c.id WHERE d.id = ?' : 'SELECT d.*, c.business_name FROM INBOUND_DDTS d JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON d.customer_id = c.id WHERE d.ddt_number = ?';
      const [ddts] = await db.query(queryStr, [identifier]);
      
      if (ddts.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'DDT non trovato' });
      const ddt = ddts[0];

      const [items] = await db.query(`
        SELECT i.*, p.sku, p.name as product_name, p.uom as product_uom
        FROM INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS i
        JOIN PRODUCTS p ON i.product_id = p.id
        WHERE i.inbound_ddt_id = ?
      `, [ddt.id]);

      const doc = new PDFDocument({ size: 'A4', margin: 40 });
      reply.header('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
      reply.header('Content-Disposition', `inline; filename="${ddt.ddt_number}.pdf"`);
      reply.send(doc);

      const fsModule = require('fs');
      const path = require('path');
      const logoPath = path.join(__dirname, '../../../frontend/public/logo.png');
      
      if (fsModule.existsSync(logoPath)) {
        doc.image(logoPath, (doc.page.width - 200) / 2, doc.y, { fit: [200, 60], align: 'center' });
        doc.moveDown(4);
      } else {
        doc.fillColor('#0ea5e9').fontSize(24).font('Helvetica-Bold').text('Logistic Porcelli', { align: 'center' });
        doc.fillColor('#94a3b8').fontSize(10).font('Helvetica').text('Logistica & Magazzino', { align: 'center' });
      }
      doc.moveDown(2);

      doc.fillColor('#1e293b').fontSize(16).font('Helvetica-Bold').text('DDT ENTRATA MERCE (SPUNTA)', { align: 'center' });
      
      // We need a barcode for the DDT number!
      const bwipjs = require('bwip-js');
      try {
        const barcodeBuffer = await bwipjs.toBuffer({ bcid: 'code128', text: ddt.ddt_number, scale: 3, height: 10, includetext: false });
        doc.image(barcodeBuffer, 225, doc.y + 10, { width: 150 });
        doc.moveDown(4);
      } catch (e) {
        doc.moveDown(2);
      }
      
      doc.fontSize(12).text(`DDT Numero: ${ddt.ddt_number}`, { align: 'center' });
      doc.moveDown(1.5);
      
      doc.fontSize(12).font('Helvetica').text(`Cliente: ${ddt.business_name}`);
      doc.text(`Data: ${new Date(ddt.created_at).toLocaleDateString('it-IT')}`);
      doc.text(`Stato: ${ddt.status}`);
      if (ddt.notes) doc.text(`Note: ${ddt.notes}`);
      
      doc.moveDown(2);
      doc.font('Helvetica-Bold').text('RIGHE ATTESE:', { underline: true });
      doc.moveDown(0.5);

      items.forEach((item, idx) => {
        const exp = parseFloat(item.expected_quantity);
        const act = parseFloat(item.actual_quantity || 0);
        const isDiscrepancy = exp !== act && ddt.status !== 'PENDING';
        
        doc.font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(11).fillColor('black').text(`${idx + 1}. ${item.product_name} (${item.sku})`);
        
        if (isDiscrepancy) {
          doc.font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(10).fillColor('#ef4444').text(`Q.ta Attesa: ${item.expected_quantity} ${item.product_uom} (Effettiva: ${item.actual_quantity}) - DISCREPANZA!`);
        } else {
          doc.font('Helvetica').fontSize(10).fillColor('#475569').text(`Q.ta Attesa: ${item.expected_quantity} ${item.product_uom} (Effettiva: ${item.actual_quantity})`);
        }
        
        doc.fillColor('black');
        if (item.batch) doc.text(`Lotto: ${item.batch}`);
        doc.moveDown(0.5);
      });

      doc.end();
      return reply;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore generazione PDF DDT' });
    }
  });


  // 7. Get Barcodes for generated pallets
  fastify.get('/ddt/:identifier/barcodes', async (request, reply) => {
    const { identifier } = request.params;
    try {
      const isId = !isNaN(identifier);
      const queryStr = isId ? 'SELECT d.id FROM INBOUND_DDTS d WHERE d.id = ?' : 'SELECT d.id FROM INBOUND_DDTS d WHERE d.ddt_number = ?';
      const [ddts] = await db.query(queryStr, [identifier]);
      if (ddts.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'DDT non trovato' });

      const [pallets] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.*, pr.name as product_name, pr.uom as product_uom, c.business_name
        FROM PALLETS p
        JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON p.customer_id = c.id
        WHERE p.inbound_ddt_id = ?
        ORDER BY p.id ASC
      `, [ddts[0].id]);

      if (pallets.length === 0) return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Nessuna paletta generata per questo DDT' });

      const groupedByCode = new Map();
      for (const p of pallets) {
        if (!groupedByCode.has(p.pallet_code)) {
          groupedByCode.set(p.pallet_code, []);
        }
        groupedByCode.get(p.pallet_code).push(p);
      }

      const generatedLabels = [];
      for (const [code, rows] of groupedByCode.entries()) {
        const p = rows[0];
        const uniqueProdNames = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.product_name))];
        const uniqueBatches = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.batch).filter(Boolean))];
        const uniqueArticles = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.client_article_number).filter(Boolean))];
        generatedLabels.push({
          code,
          customer_name: p.business_name,
          product_name: rows.length > 1
            ? (uniqueProdNames.length === 1 ? `${uniqueProdNames[0]} (${rows.length} LOTTI)` : `PALETTA MISTA (${rows.length} Articoli)`)
            : p.product_name,
          quantity: rows.length > 1 ? '-' : p.quantity,
          uom: p.pallet_uom || p.product_uom || 'Pezzi',
          batch: rows.length > 1 ? (uniqueBatches.join(', ') || '-') : (p.batch || '-'),
          warehouse: p.warehouse,
          client_pallet_number: p.client_pallet_number,
          client_article_number: rows.length > 1 ? (uniqueArticles.join(', ') || '-') : p.client_article_number,
          expiration_date: p.expiration_date,
          location: p.location || null,
          flags: p.flags
        });
      }

      const pdfBuffer = await generateLabelsPdfBuffer(generatedLabels, { paper_format: 'A4', print_mode: 'GRID' });
      reply.header('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
      reply.header('Content-Disposition', 'inline; filename="barcodes.pdf"');
      return reply.send(pdfBuffer);
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore generazione barcodes' });
    }
  });

}

module.exports = inboundRoutes;

const db = require('../config/db');
const PDFDocument = require('pdfkit');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const logoPath = path.join(__dirname, '../../../frontend/public/logo.png');

async function locationRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  // GET /api/locations
  fastify.get('/', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT l.*, 
               GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT p.pallet_code SEPARATOR ', ') as pallet_code,
               GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT pr.name SEPARATOR ', ') as product_name
        FROM LOCATIONS l
        LEFT JOIN PALLETS p ON l.barcode = p.location AND p.status = 'STOCKED'
        LEFT JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
        GROUP BY l.id
        ORDER BY l.zone ASC, l.col ASC, l.pos ASC
      `);
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante il recupero delle posizioni' });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/locations/routing-zones
  fastify.get('/routing-zones', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      // Zone classiche
      const [classicZones] = await db.query('SELECT DISTINCT zone FROM LOCATIONS WHERE zone IS NOT NULL');
      const zones = classicZones.map(z => z.zone);

      // Free Locations dalla tabella
      const [freeLocRows] = await db.query('SELECT name FROM FREE_LOCATIONS');
      
      // Free Locations (nomi location legacy dalle palette che non sono in LOCATIONS, escludendo ZONA CARICO temporanea)
      const [legacyFreeLocations] = await db.query(`
        SELECT DISTINCT p.location 
        FROM PALLETS p 
        LEFT JOIN LOCATIONS l ON p.location = l.barcode 
        WHERE l.barcode IS NULL AND p.location IS NOT NULL AND p.location != '' AND p.location NOT LIKE 'ZONA CARICO%'
      `);
      
      const allZones = [...new Set([
        ...zones, 
        ...freeLocRows.map(f => f.name),
        ...legacyFreeLocations.map(f => f.location)
      ])];
      return allZones;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore recupero zone per routing' });
    }
  });

  // POST /api/locations/bulk - Generatore massivo
  fastify.post('/bulk', async (request, reply) => {
    const { zone, rack, levels } = request.body;
    
    if (!zone || !rack || !levels || levels.length === 0) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Dati incompleti per la generazione' });
    }

    const connection = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();
      let created = 0;

      // Recuperiamo i PIN già in uso per questa zona per garantire l'univocità
      const [existing] = await connection.query('SELECT pin FROM LOCATIONS WHERE zone = ?', [zone.toUpperCase()]);
      const usedPins = new Set(existing.map(r => r.pin));

      for (const pos of levels) {
        const barcode = `${zone.toUpperCase()}-${rack.toUpperCase()}-${pos}`;
        
        let pin;
        // Genera un PIN univoco (da 100 a 999 per avere più combinazioni disponibili)
        do {
          pin = Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900).toString();
        } while (usedPins.has(pin));
        
        usedPins.add(pin);
        
        try {
          await connection.query(
            'INSERT INTO LOCATIONS (zone, col, pos, barcode, pin) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
            [zone.toUpperCase(), rack.toUpperCase(), pos, barcode, pin]
          );
          await connection.query(
            'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
            ['CREATE_LOC', `Creata posizione ${barcode}`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
          );
          created++;
        } catch (e) {
          // Se esiste già (Duplicate entry), ignora
          if (e.code !== 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') throw e;
        }
      }

      if (fastify.io) {
        fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');
      }

      await connection.commit();
      connection.release();
      return { success: true, message: `Generate ${created} nuove posizioni.` };
    } catch (err) {
      if (connection) {
        await connection.rollback();
        connection.release();
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante la generazione massiva' });
    }
  });

  // DELETE /api/locations/:id
  fastify.delete('/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    const { id } = request.params;
    
    if (request.user.role !== 'developer') {
      return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Azione consentita solo ai developer' });
    }

    try {
      const [[loc]] = await db.query('SELECT barcode FROM LOCATIONS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      if (!loc) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Posizione non trovata' });

      const [pallets] = await db.query("SELECT id FROM PALLETS WHERE location = ? AND status = 'STOCKED'", [loc.barcode]);
      if (pallets.length > 0) {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Impossibile eliminare: la posizione è attualmente occupata da una paletta!' });
      }

      await db.query('DELETE FROM LOCATIONS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['DELETE_LOC', `Eliminata posizione ${loc.barcode}`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante l\'eliminazione' });
    }
  });

  // DELETE /api/locations/zone/:zoneName
  fastify.delete('/zone/:zoneName', async (request, reply) => {
    const { zoneName } = request.params;
    
    if (request.user.role !== 'developer') {
      return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Azione consentita solo ai developer' });
    }

    try {
      // Controllo se ci sono palette nella zona (attraverso la foreign key fittizia o la join)
      const [occupied] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.id 
        FROM PALLETS p
        JOIN LOCATIONS l ON p.location = l.barcode
        WHERE l.zone = ? AND p.status = 'STOCKED'
        LIMIT 1
      `, [zoneName]);

      if (occupied.length > 0) {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Impossibile eliminare la zona: alcune posizioni sono occupate.' });
      }

      await db.query('DELETE FROM LOCATIONS WHERE zone = ?', [zoneName]);
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['DELETE_ZONE', `Eliminata intera zona ${zoneName}`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante l\'eliminazione della zona' });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/locations/zone/:zoneName/print
  fastify.get('/zone/:zoneName/print', async (request, reply) => {
    const { zoneName } = request.params;
    try {
      const [locations] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM LOCATIONS WHERE zone = ? ORDER BY col ASC, pos ASC', [zoneName]);
      
      if (locations.length === 0) {
        return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Nessuna posizione trovata nella zona' });
      }

      const doc = new PDFDocument({ size: 'A4', margin: 20 });
      reply.header('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
      reply.header('Content-Disposition', `inline; filename="PIN_${zoneName}.pdf"`);
      reply.send(doc);

      const labelsPerRow = 2;
      const labelsPerCol = 4;
      const labelWidth = (doc.page.width - 40) / labelsPerRow; // ~277
      const labelHeight = (doc.page.height - 40) / labelsPerCol; // ~200

      for (let i = 0; i < locations.length; i++) {
        const loc = locations[i];
        const pageIndex = i % (labelsPerRow * labelsPerCol);
        if (i > 0 && pageIndex === 0) {
          doc.addPage();
        }

        const col = pageIndex % labelsPerRow;
        const row = Math.floor(pageIndex / labelsPerRow);
        
        const x = 20 + (col * labelWidth);
        const y = 20 + (row * labelHeight);

        // Bordo etichetta
        doc.rect(x + 5, y + 5, labelWidth - 10, labelHeight - 10).stroke('#cccccc');

        // Logo
        if (fs.existsSync(logoPath)) {
          doc.image(logoPath, x + 10, y + 10, { fit: [60, 20] });
        }

        // Scaffale/Posizione (In alto)
        doc.font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(16).fillColor('#333333').text(String(loc.barcode || ''), x, y + 35, {
          width: labelWidth,
          align: 'center'
        });

        // PIN Gigantesco (Centro) (Auto-shrink text se per assurdo fosse lunghissimo)
        let pinStr = String(loc.pin || '');
        let pinSize = 90;
        doc.font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(pinSize);
        while(doc.widthOfString(pinStr) > (labelWidth - 20) && pinSize > 20) {
          pinSize -= 5;
          doc.fontSize(pinSize);
        }
        
        doc.fillColor('#000000').text(pinStr, x, y + 70, {
          width: labelWidth,
          align: 'center'
        });
        
        // Info testuale aggiuntiva (In basso)
        doc.font('Helvetica').fontSize(10).fillColor('#666666').text(`Zona: ${loc.zone || ''} | Col: ${loc.col || ''} | Liv: ${loc.pos || ''}`, x, y + 175, {
          width: labelWidth,
          align: 'center'
        });
      }
      
      doc.end();
      return reply;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante la generazione del PDF' });
    }
  });

  fastify.get('/free', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM FREE_LOCATIONS ORDER BY name ASC');
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore fetch free locations' });
    }
  });

  fastify.post('/free', async (request, reply) => {
    const { name } = request.body;
    if (!name || name.trim() === '') return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Nome obbligatorio' });
    try {
      await db.query('INSERT INTO FREE_LOCATIONS (name) VALUES (?)', [name.trim().toUpperCase()]);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore creazione free location (forse nome duplicato?)' });
    }
  });

  fastify.delete('/free/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    const { id } = request.params;
    try {
      await db.query('DELETE FROM FREE_LOCATIONS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore eliminazione free location' });
    }
  });
}

=== FILE: backend/src/routes/outbound.js ===
const db = require('../config/db');
const PDFDocument = require('pdfkit');
const bwipjs = require('bwip-js');

async function getRoutingSettings(connection) {
  const [rows] = await connection.query('SELECT setting_key, setting_value FROM settings WHERE setting_key IN ("zone_routing_priority", "position_routing_priority")');
  let zonePriority = [];
  let posPriority = ['01,02,03', '11,12,13', '21,22,23'];
  rows.forEach(r => {
    try {
      if (r.setting_key === 'zone_routing_priority' && r.setting_value) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(r.setting_value);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) zonePriority = parsed;
      }
      if (r.setting_key === 'position_routing_priority' && r.setting_value) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(r.setting_value);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) posPriority = parsed;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // ignore malformed JSON
    }
  });
  return { zonePriority, posPriority };
}

function extractLocationCoords(item) {
  let zone = (item.zone && item.zone !== 'null') ? String(item.zone).trim().toUpperCase() : '';
  let col = (item.col && item.col !== 'null') ? String(item.col).trim().toUpperCase() : '';
  let pos = (item.pos && item.pos !== 'null') ? String(item.pos).trim().toUpperCase() : '';
  const rawLoc = (item.location && item.location !== 'null') ? String(item.location).trim().toUpperCase() : '';

  if (!zone && rawLoc) {
    const parts = rawLoc.split('-').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (parts.length >= 3) {
      zone = parts[0];
      col = parts[1];
      pos = parts.slice(2).join('-');
    } else {
      zone = rawLoc;
    }
  }
  return { zone, col, pos, rawLoc };
}

function getZoneSortRank(zone, zonePriority) {
  if (!zone) return 9999;
  const normZone = zone.trim().toUpperCase();
  const normPriority = (zonePriority || []).map(z => String(z).trim().toUpperCase());
  const idx = normPriority.indexOf(normZone);
  return idx >= 0 ? idx : 999;
}

function getPosSortRank(pos, posPriority) {
  if (!pos) return { groupIdx: 999, subIdx: 999 };
  const normPos = String(pos).trim().toUpperCase();
  const patterns = Array.isArray(posPriority) ? posPriority : [];

  // 1. Ricerca esatta nel pattern configurato (es. "01,02,03")
  for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i++) {
    const tokens = String(patterns[i]).split(',').map(s => s.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
    const subIdx = tokens.indexOf(normPos);
    if (subIdx >= 0) {
      return { groupIdx: i, subIdx };
    }
  }

  // 2. Fallback intelligente per stesso piano (es. pos "04" quando il gruppo 0 ha "01,02,03" -> piano "0")
  if (normPos.length >= 2 && /^\d+$/.test(normPos)) {
    const floorPrefix = normPos[0];
    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i++) {
      const tokens = String(patterns[i]).split(',').map(s => s.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
      if (tokens.length > 0 && tokens.every(t => t.length >= 2 && t[0] === floorPrefix)) {
        return { groupIdx: i, subIdx: 100 + parseInt(normPos, 10) };
      }
    }
  }

  return { groupIdx: 999, subIdx: 999 };
}

// Confronto puro per percorso fisico in magazzino (Regole di Prelievo senza avanti-e-indietro)
function compareByWarehousePath(a, b, zonePriority, posPriority) {
  const coordsA = extractLocationCoords(a);
  const coordsB = extractLocationCoords(b);

  // 1. Priorità Zona (ordine impostato in Regole di Prelievo)
  const zA = getZoneSortRank(coordsA.zone, zonePriority);
  const zB = getZoneSortRank(coordsB.zone, zonePriority);
  if (zA !== zB) return zA - zB;

  // Se entrambe le zone non sono nella lista priorità, raggruppa per nome zona
  if (coordsA.zone !== coordsB.zone) {
    return coordsA.zone.localeCompare(coordsB.zone, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
  }

  // 2. Stessa Zona -> Ordina per Corsia / Scaffale (col: A, B, C... o 01, 02...) per non fare avanti e indietro tra scaffali
  if (coordsA.col !== coordsB.col) {
    if (!coordsA.col) return 1;
    if (!coordsB.col) return -1;
    return coordsA.col.localeCompare(coordsB.col, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
  }

  // 3. Stesso Scaffale -> Priorità Piano / Posizione (es. prima piano terra 01,02,03 in ordine, poi 11,12,13...)
  const pA = getPosSortRank(coordsA.pos, posPriority);
  const pB = getPosSortRank(coordsB.pos, posPriority);
  if (pA.groupIdx !== pB.groupIdx) return pA.groupIdx - pB.groupIdx;
  if (pA.subIdx !== pB.subIdx) return pA.subIdx - pB.subIdx;

  if (coordsA.pos !== coordsB.pos) {
    return coordsA.pos.localeCompare(coordsB.pos, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
  }

  // 4. Stessa Posizione -> tieni vicine le stesse locazioni e stesse palette
  if (coordsA.rawLoc !== coordsB.rawLoc) {
    return coordsA.rawLoc.localeCompare(coordsB.rawLoc, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
  }

  const palA = String(a.pallet_code || '');
  const palB = String(b.pallet_code || '');
  if (palA !== palB) {
    return palA.localeCompare(palB, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
  }

  return (a.id || 0) - (b.id || 0);
}

// Ordinamento per allocazione merce (stesso prodotto: FEFO scadenza -> Regole di Prelievo)
function sortPalletsByRouting(pallets, zonePriority, posPriority) {
  return pallets.sort((a, b) => {
    const expA = a.expiration_date ? new Date(a.expiration_date).getTime() : 9999999999999;
    const expB = b.expiration_date ? new Date(b.expiration_date).getTime() : 9999999999999;
    if (expA !== expB) return expA - expB;

    return compareByWarehousePath(a, b, zonePriority, posPriority);
  });
}

// Ordinamento percorso di prelievo per Zebra e Picking List PDF (rispetta Regole di Prelievo senza avanti e indietro)
function sortPickingTour(items, zonePriority, posPriority) {
  return items.sort((a, b) => compareByWarehousePath(a, b, zonePriority, posPriority));
}

async function allocateOutboundItems(items, connection, excludeOrderId = null) {
  const allocatedPallets = [];
  const { zonePriority, posPriority } = await getRoutingSettings(connection);
  const inMemoryUsedByPalletId = new Map();

  let excludeCondition = '';
  if (excludeOrderId) {
    excludeCondition = `AND oo.id != ${connection.escape(excludeOrderId)}`;
  }

  for (const req of items) {
    const normalizedUom = req.requested_uom === 'Palette' ? 'Bancali' : (req.requested_uom || 'Pezzi');

    // Se è già indicato pallet_code (es. in modifica ordine su riga già allocata)
    if (req.pallet_code) {
      let resolvedPalletId = req.pallet_id || null;
      let resolvedBatch = (req.batch && req.batch !== 'TUTTI' && req.batch !== 'Nessun lotto') ? req.batch : null;

      if (!resolvedPalletId) {
        let findSql = 'SELECT id, batch FROM PALLETS WHERE pallet_code = ? AND product_id = ?';
        const findParams = [req.pallet_code, req.product_id];
        if (resolvedBatch) {
          findSql += ' AND TRIM(batch) = TRIM(?)';
          findParams.push(resolvedBatch);
        } else if (req.batch === 'Nessun lotto') {
          findSql += " AND (batch IS NULL OR TRIM(batch) = '')";
        }
        findSql += ' ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT 1';
        const [foundRows] = await connection.query(findSql, findParams);
        if (foundRows.length > 0) {
          resolvedPalletId = foundRows[0].id;
          resolvedBatch = foundRows[0].batch || null;
        }
      }

      allocatedPallets.push({
        ...req,
        pallet_id: resolvedPalletId,
        batch: resolvedBatch,
        requested_uom: normalizedUom
      });
      continue;
    }

    let query = `
      SELECT pal.*, loc.zone, loc.col, loc.pos,
             COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) as effective_units_per_box,
             COALESCE(p.boxes_per_pallet, 1) as effective_boxes_per_pallet,
             (pal.quantity - COALESCE((
               SELECT SUM(
                 CASE 
                   WHEN oi.requested_uom IN ('Bancali', 'Palette') THEN 
                     CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.boxes_per_pallet, 1) > 1 AND pal.quantity >= 2 * (COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) * p.boxes_per_pallet)
                          THEN (oi.quantity_required * COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) * p.boxes_per_pallet)
                          ELSE pal.quantity 
                     END
                   WHEN oi.requested_uom IN ('Scatole', 'Cartoni') THEN (oi.quantity_required * COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1))
                   ELSE oi.quantity_required 
                 END
               )
               FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS oi
               JOIN OUTBOUND_ORDERS oo ON oi.order_id = oo.id
               WHERE (
                 (oi.pallet_id IS NOT NULL AND oi.pallet_id = pal.id)
                 OR (oi.pallet_id IS NULL AND oi.pallet_code = pal.pallet_code AND oi.product_id = pal.product_id AND (oi.batch IS NULL OR oi.batch = pal.batch))
               )
               AND oo.status != 'SHIPPED'
               ${excludeCondition}
             ), 0)) as available_quantity
      FROM PALLETS pal 
      JOIN PRODUCTS p ON pal.product_id = p.id 
      LEFT JOIN LOCATIONS loc ON pal.location = loc.barcode 
      WHERE pal.product_id = ? AND pal.status != 'SHIPPED' AND pal.quantity > 0
    `;
    const params = [req.product_id];
    if (req.batch && req.batch !== 'TUTTI' && req.batch !== 'Nessun lotto') {
      query += ` AND TRIM(pal.batch) = TRIM(?)`;
      params.push(req.batch);
    } else if (req.batch === 'Nessun lotto') {
      query += ` AND (pal.batch IS NULL OR TRIM(pal.batch) = '')`;
    }
    
    const [rawAvailable] = await connection.query(query, params);
    const available = rawAvailable.filter(pal => {
      const alreadyUsedBase = inMemoryUsedByPalletId.get(pal.id) || 0;
      return (parseFloat(pal.available_quantity) - alreadyUsedBase) > 0;
    });
    sortPalletsByRouting(available, zonePriority, posPriority);

    let remainingToAllocate = parseFloat(req.quantity_required);
    const allocatedForThisReq = [];

    for (const pal of available) {
      if (remainingToAllocate <= 0) break;

      const alreadyUsedBase = inMemoryUsedByPalletId.get(pal.id) || 0;
      const availBaseQty = Math.max(0, parseFloat(pal.available_quantity) - alreadyUsedBase);
      if (availBaseQty <= 0) continue;

      const upb = parseFloat(pal.effective_units_per_box) || 1;
      const bpp = parseFloat(pal.effective_boxes_per_pallet) || 1;
      const unitsPerPallet = upb * bpp;

      let palletQtyInRequestedUom = availBaseQty;
      if (normalizedUom === 'Scatole' || normalizedUom === 'Cartoni') {
        palletQtyInRequestedUom = availBaseQty / upb;
      } else if (normalizedUom === 'Bancali') {
        // Se la riga paletta rappresenta più bancali accorpati (es. carico multi-paletta su stessa posizione)
        // e non ci sono abbastanza righe distinte per coprire la richiesta, calcola i bancali contenuti nella riga
        if (available.length < remainingToAllocate && unitsPerPallet > 1 && availBaseQty >= unitsPerPallet * 2) {
          palletQtyInRequestedUom = Math.max(1, Math.floor(availBaseQty / unitsPerPallet));
        } else {
          palletQtyInRequestedUom = 1;
        }
      }

      if (palletQtyInRequestedUom <= 0) continue;

      const take = Math.min(remainingToAllocate, palletQtyInRequestedUom);
      
      allocatedForThisReq.push({
        pallet_id: pal.id,
        pallet_code: pal.pallet_code,
        product_id: req.product_id,
        batch: pal.batch || null,
        quantity_required: take,
        requested_uom: normalizedUom,
        location: pal.location || null,
        zone: pal.zone || null,
        col: pal.col || null,
        pos: pal.pos || null
      });

      let usedBaseForTake = take;
      if (normalizedUom === 'Scatole' || normalizedUom === 'Cartoni') {
        usedBaseForTake = take * upb;
      } else if (normalizedUom === 'Bancali') {
        usedBaseForTake = (palletQtyInRequestedUom > 1 && unitsPerPallet > 1) ? (take * unitsPerPallet) : availBaseQty;
      }
      inMemoryUsedByPalletId.set(pal.id, alreadyUsedBase + usedBaseForTake);
      
      remainingToAllocate -= take;
    }

    if (remainingToAllocate > 0) {
      const batchLabel = (req.batch && req.batch !== 'TUTTI') ? ` (Lotto: ${req.batch})` : '';
      throw new Error(`Quantità insufficiente in magazzino per l'articolo richiesto${batchLabel}`);
    }
    
    allocatedPallets.push(...allocatedForThisReq);
  }
  sortPickingTour(allocatedPallets, zonePriority, posPriority);
  return allocatedPallets;
}

async function outboundRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  // GET /api/outbound - List all orders
  fastify.get('/', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [orders] = await db.query(`
        SELECT o.*, c.business_name as customer_name,
               (SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT COALESCE(oi.batch, pal.batch) SEPARATOR ', ')
                FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS oi
                LEFT JOIN PALLETS pal ON pal.id = oi.pallet_id
                WHERE oi.order_id = o.id) as batches,
               (SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT p.name SEPARATOR ', ')
                FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS oi
                JOIN PRODUCTS p ON p.id = oi.product_id
                WHERE oi.order_id = o.id) as products,
               (SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT oi.pallet_code SEPARATOR ', ')
                FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS oi
                WHERE oi.order_id = o.id) as pallets
        FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS o
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON o.customer_id = c.id
        ORDER BY o.created_at DESC
      `);
      return orders;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore nel recupero degli ordini' });
    }
  });

  // POST /api/outbound - Create new order
  fastify.post('/', async (request, reply) => {
    const { customer_id, exit_date, items, client_ddt } = request.body;
    const connection = await db.getConnection();
    
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();
      
      const orderCode = `OUT-${new Date().toISOString().slice(2,10).replace(/-/g, '')}-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;
      
      const username = request.user?.username || 'SYSTEM';
      
      const [orderRes] = await connection.query(
        'INSERT INTO OUTBOUND_ORDERS (order_code, customer_id, exit_date, created_by, client_ddt) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
        [orderCode, customer_id ?? null, exit_date ?? null, username, client_ddt ?? null]
      );
      const orderId = orderRes.insertId;

      const allocatedItemsPOST = await allocateOutboundItems(items, connection);
      for (const item of allocatedItemsPOST) {
        await connection.query(
          'INSERT INTO OUTBOUND_ITEMS (order_id, pallet_id, pallet_code, product_id, batch, quantity_required, requested_uom) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
          [orderId, item.pallet_id ?? null, item.pallet_code ?? null, item.product_id ?? null, item.batch ?? null, item.quantity_required ?? null, item.requested_uom ?? null]
        );
      }
      
      // Audit Log
      await connection.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['CREATE_OUTBOUND', `Creata spedizione ${orderCode}`, 'BACKOFFICE', username]
      );

      fastify.io.emit('audit_log', {
        action: 'CREATE_OUTBOUND',
        details: `Creata spedizione ${orderCode}`,
        source: 'BACKOFFICE',
        username: username,
        created_at: new Date()
      });

      await connection.commit();
      connection.release();
      return { success: true, order_code: orderCode };
    } catch (err) {
      if (connection) {
        await connection.rollback();
        connection.release();
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: err.message || "Errore nella creazione dell'ordine" });
    }
  });

  // PUT /api/outbound/:id - Edit an outbound order
  fastify.put('/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    const { id } = request.params;
    const { customer_id, exit_date, items, client_ddt } = request.body;
    
    const userRole = request.user?.role;
    if (userRole !== 'developer' && userRole !== 'backoffice' && userRole !== 'admin') {
      return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Non autorizzato a modificare le spedizioni' });
    }

    const connection = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();
      
      const [orders] = await connection.query('SELECT status, order_code FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      if (orders.length === 0) throw new Error('Ordine non trovato');
      if (orders[0].status === 'SHIPPED') throw new Error('Impossibile modificare un ordine già spedito.');

      await connection.query(
        'UPDATE OUTBOUND_ORDERS SET customer_id = ?, exit_date = ?, client_ddt = ?, status = "PENDING" WHERE id = ?',
        [customer_id ?? null, exit_date ?? null, client_ddt ?? null, id]
      );

      await connection.query('DELETE FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS WHERE order_id = ?', [id]);

      const allocatedItemsPUT = await allocateOutboundItems(items, connection, id);
      for (const item of allocatedItemsPUT) {
        await connection.query(
          'INSERT INTO OUTBOUND_ITEMS (order_id, pallet_id, pallet_code, product_id, batch, quantity_required, requested_uom) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
          [id, item.pallet_id ?? null, item.pallet_code ?? null, item.product_id ?? null, item.batch ?? null, item.quantity_required ?? null, item.requested_uom ?? null]
        );
      }
      
      const orderCode = orders[0].order_code;
      const username = request.user?.username || 'SYSTEM';

      // Audit Log
      await connection.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['UPDATE_OUTBOUND', `Modificata spedizione ${orderCode}`, 'BACKOFFICE', username]
      );

      fastify.io.emit('audit_log', {
        action: 'UPDATE_OUTBOUND',
        details: `Modificata spedizione ${orderCode}`,
        source: 'BACKOFFICE',
        username: username,
        created_at: new Date()
      });

      await connection.commit();
      connection.release();
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      if (connection) {
        await connection.rollback();
        connection.release();
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
