      return reply.code(500).send({ error: err.message || "Errore nella modifica dell'ordine" });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/outbound/:id/details - Get order details for editing
  fastify.get('/:id/details', async (request, reply) => {
    const { id } = request.params;
    try {
      const [orders] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      if (orders.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Ordine non trovato' });
      
      const [items] = await db.query(`
        SELECT i.*, p.name as product_name, p.uom as base_uom, COALESCE(i.batch, pal.batch) as batch, pal.location
        FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS i
        JOIN PRODUCTS p ON i.product_id = p.id
        LEFT JOIN PALLETS pal ON pal.id = COALESCE(
          i.pallet_id,
          (SELECT p2.id FROM PALLETS p2 
           WHERE p2.pallet_code = i.pallet_code 
             AND p2.product_id = i.product_id 
             AND (i.batch IS NULL OR p2.batch = i.batch)
           ORDER BY p2.id ASC LIMIT 1)
        )
        WHERE i.order_id = ?
      `, [id]);
      
      return { order: orders[0], items };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore nel recupero dettagli ordine' });
    }
  });

  // DELETE /api/outbound/:id - Delete an outbound order (Developer / Backoffice)
  fastify.delete('/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    const { id } = request.params;
    
    // Auth check
    const userRole = request.user?.role;
    if (userRole !== 'developer' && userRole !== 'backoffice' && userRole !== 'admin') {
      return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Non autorizzato a eliminare le spedizioni' });
    }

    try {
      const [orders] = await db.query('SELECT status, order_code FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      if (orders.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Ordine non trovato' });
      
      const order = orders[0];
      if (order.status === 'SHIPPED') {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Impossibile eliminare un ordine già confermato e spedito. Le giacenze sono già state scaricate.' });
      }

      await db.query('DELETE FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS WHERE id = ?', [id]);
      
      const orderCode = order.order_code;
      const username = request.user?.username || 'SYSTEM';
      
      // Audit Log
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['DELETE_OUTBOUND', `Eliminata spedizione ${orderCode}`, 'BACKOFFICE', username]
      );

      fastify.io.emit('audit_log', {
        action: 'DELETE_OUTBOUND',
        details: `Eliminata spedizione ${orderCode}`,
        source: 'BACKOFFICE',
        username: username,
        created_at: new Date()
      });

      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: "Errore nell'eliminazione dell'ordine" });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/outbound/:code/pdf - Print DDT / Picking List
  fastify.get('/:code/pdf', async (request, reply) => {
    const { code } = request.params;
    try {
      const [orders] = await db.query(`
        SELECT o.*, c.business_name as customer_name
        FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS o
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON o.customer_id = c.id
        WHERE o.order_code = ?
      `, [code]);
      
      if (orders.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Ordine non trovato' });
      const order = orders[0];

      const [items] = await db.query(`
        SELECT i.*, p.name as product_name, COALESCE(i.requested_uom, p.uom) as uom, COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) as units_per_box, pal.location, COALESCE(i.batch, pal.batch) as batch, pal.expiration_date, loc.zone, loc.col, loc.pos, loc.pin
        FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS i
        JOIN PRODUCTS p ON i.product_id = p.id
        LEFT JOIN PALLETS pal ON pal.id = COALESCE(
          i.pallet_id,
          (SELECT p2.id FROM PALLETS p2 
           WHERE p2.pallet_code = i.pallet_code 
             AND p2.product_id = i.product_id 
             AND (i.batch IS NULL OR p2.batch = i.batch)
           ORDER BY p2.id ASC LIMIT 1)
        )
        LEFT JOIN LOCATIONS loc ON pal.location = loc.barcode
        WHERE i.order_id = ?
      `, [order.id]);

      const connection = await db.getConnection();
      try {
        const { zonePriority, posPriority } = await getRoutingSettings(connection);
        sortPickingTour(items, zonePriority, posPriority);
      } finally {
        connection.release();
      }

      const doc = new PDFDocument({ size: 'A4', margin: 40 });
      reply.header('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
      reply.header('Content-Disposition', `inline; filename="DDT_${code}.pdf"`);
      reply.send(doc);

      // --- BRAND HEADER ---
      const fs = require('fs');
      const path = require('path');
      const logoPath = path.join(__dirname, '../../../frontend/public/logo.png');
      
      if (fs.existsSync(logoPath)) {
        doc.image(logoPath, 40, 30, { fit: [150, 50] });
      } else {
        doc.fillColor('#192935').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(24).text('LOGISTIC PORCELLI', 40, 35, { align: 'left' });
      }
      
      doc.fillColor('#64748b').fontSize(10).font('Helvetica').text('Magazzino e Logistica Avanzata', 350, 45, { align: 'right', width: 200 });
      doc.moveDown(4);

      // --- DOCUMENT TITLE ---
      doc.y = 95;
      doc.fillColor('#192935').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(16).text('DOCUMENTO DI TRASPORTO INTERNO (PICKING LIST)', 40, doc.y, { align: 'left' });
      doc.moveTo(40, doc.y + 5).lineTo(550, doc.y + 5).lineWidth(2).strokeColor('#192935').stroke();
      doc.moveDown(1.5);

      // --- INFO CARDS ---
      const infoTop = doc.y;
      doc.lineWidth(1);
      
      // Box Cliente
      doc.rect(40, infoTop, 245, 60).fillAndStroke('#f8fafc', '#e2e8f0');
      doc.fillColor('#64748b').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(9).text('CLIENTE DESTINATARIO:', 50, infoTop + 10);
      
      // Auto-shrink nome cliente
      let cName = (order.customer_name || '').toUpperCase();
      let maxFontSizeC = 14;
      doc.fontSize(maxFontSizeC).font('Helvetica-Bold');
      while (doc.widthOfString(cName) > 225 && maxFontSizeC > 8) {
        maxFontSizeC -= 1;
        doc.fontSize(maxFontSizeC);
      }
      doc.fillColor('#192935').text(cName, 50, infoTop + 25);

      // Box Spedizione
      doc.rect(305, infoTop, 245, 60).fillAndStroke('#f8fafc', '#e2e8f0');
      doc.fillColor('#64748b').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(9).text('DATA PREVISTA USCITA:', 315, infoTop + 10);
      doc.fillColor('#192935').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(14).text(new Date(order.exit_date).toLocaleDateString('it-IT'), 315, infoTop + 25);

      doc.y = infoTop + 75;
      
      const detailsLines = [];
      if (order.client_ddt) {
        detailsLines.push(`RIF. DDT CLIENTE: ${order.client_ddt}`);
      }
      if (order.created_by) {
        detailsLines.push(`PREPARATO DA: ${order.created_by}`);
      }

      if (detailsLines.length > 0) {
        doc.fillColor('#192935').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(11).text(detailsLines.join('   |   '), { align: 'center' });
        doc.moveDown(0.5);
      }

      // --- BARCODE SECTION ---
      doc.fillColor('#192935').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(12).text('CODICE ORDINE: ' + order.order_code, { align: 'center' });
      const barcodeBuffer = await bwipjs.toBuffer({
        bcid: 'code128',
        text: order.order_code,
        scale: 3,
        height: 12,
        includetext: true,
        textxalign: 'center',
      });
      doc.image(barcodeBuffer, (doc.page.width - 200) / 2, doc.y + 5, { width: 200 });
      doc.moveDown(5);
      
      doc.fillColor('#64748b').font('Helvetica').fontSize(10).text('Da scansionare con terminale Zebra per avviare il prelievo guidato', { align: 'center' });
      doc.moveDown(2);

      // --- ITEMS TABLE ---
      let tableTop = doc.y;
      
      const drawTableHeader = (yPos) => {
        doc.rect(40, yPos, 515, 25).fill('#192935');
        doc.fillColor('#ffffff').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(9);
        doc.text('POSIZIONE', 45, yPos + 8);
        doc.text('ARTICOLO', 135, yPos + 8);
        doc.text('LOTTO', 265, yPos + 8);
        doc.text('PALETTA', 345, yPos + 8);
        doc.text('Q.TÀ', 485, yPos + 8);
      };

      drawTableHeader(tableTop);
      let y = tableTop + 35;

      for (const item of items) {
        let locStr = 'NO POS.';
        if (item.zone && item.zone !== 'null') {
          locStr = `${item.zone} | ${item.col} | ${item.pos}`;
        } else if (item.location && item.location !== 'null') {
          locStr = item.location;
        }

        // Calculate height for article name to allow multiline
        const articleHeight = doc.font('Helvetica').fontSize(9).heightOfString(item.product_name, { width: 120 });
        const rowHeight = Math.max(25, articleHeight + 10);

        if (y + rowHeight > 780) {
          doc.addPage();
          drawTableHeader(40);
          y = 75;
        }
        
        doc.font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(9).fillColor('#192935').text(locStr, 45, y, { width: 85 });
        doc.font('Helvetica').fontSize(9).fillColor('#334155').text(item.product_name, 135, y, { width: 120 });
        doc.text(item.batch || '-', 265, y, { width: 75 });
        
        const displayPallet = (item.pallet_code || '').startsWith('NOBAR-') ? '-' : item.pallet_code;
        doc.font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(8).fillColor('#192935').text(displayPallet, 345, y, { width: 135 });
        
        let qtyText = `${parseFloat(item.quantity_required)} ${item.uom}`;
        if (item.units_per_box > 1 && item.uom !== 'Scatole' && item.uom !== 'Bancali' && item.uom !== 'Palette') {
           const scatole = Math.floor(item.quantity_required / item.units_per_box);
           const sfusi = item.quantity_required % item.units_per_box;
           let breakDown = [];
           if (scatole > 0) breakDown.push(`${scatole} SCAT.`);
           if (sfusi > 0) breakDown.push(`${sfusi} SFUSI`);
           if (breakDown.length > 0) qtyText = `${breakDown.join(' + ')} (${parseFloat(item.quantity_required)} pz)`;
        } else if (item.units_per_box > 1 && (item.uom === 'Scatole' || item.uom === 'Cartoni')) {
           qtyText = `${parseFloat(item.quantity_required)} SCAT. (${parseFloat(item.quantity_required) * item.units_per_box} pz)`;
        }

        doc.fillColor('#192935').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(9).text(qtyText, 485, y, { width: 70 });
        
        doc.moveTo(40, y + rowHeight - 5).lineTo(555, y + rowHeight - 5).lineWidth(0.5).strokeColor('#e2e8f0').stroke();
        y += rowHeight;
      }

      doc.end();
      return reply;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore generazione DDT' });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/outbound/zebra/:code - Fetch order details for Zebra
  fastify.get('/zebra/:code', async (request, reply) => {
    const { code } = request.params;
    try {
      const [orders] = await db.query(`
        SELECT o.*, c.business_name as customer_name
        FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS o
        LEFT JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON o.customer_id = c.id
        WHERE o.order_code = ?
      `, [code]);
      if (orders.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Ordine non trovato' });
      const order = orders[0];

      if (order.status === 'SHIPPED') {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Ordine già spedito' });
      }

      const [items] = await db.query(`
        SELECT i.*, p.name as product_name, p.sku, p.uom as base_uom,
               COALESCE(pal.client_article_number, p.sku) as article_code,
               COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) as units_per_box,
               COALESCE(p.boxes_per_pallet, 1) as boxes_per_pallet,
               COALESCE(i.requested_uom, pal.pallet_uom, p.uom) as uom,
               pal.location, pal.quantity as pallet_quantity,
               COALESCE(i.batch, pal.batch) as batch, pal.expiration_date,
               loc.zone, loc.col, loc.pos, loc.pin as loc_pin
        FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS i
        JOIN PRODUCTS p ON i.product_id = p.id
        LEFT JOIN PALLETS pal ON pal.id = COALESCE(
          i.pallet_id,
          (SELECT p2.id FROM PALLETS p2 
           WHERE p2.pallet_code = i.pallet_code 
             AND p2.product_id = i.product_id 
             AND (i.batch IS NULL OR p2.batch = i.batch)
           ORDER BY p2.id ASC LIMIT 1)
        )
        LEFT JOIN LOCATIONS loc ON pal.location = loc.barcode
        WHERE i.order_id = ?
      `, [order.id]);

      const connection = await db.getConnection();
      try {
        const { zonePriority, posPriority } = await getRoutingSettings(connection);
        sortPickingTour(items, zonePriority, posPriority);
      } finally {
        connection.release();
      }

      // Imposta in PICKING se era PENDING
      if (order.status === 'PENDING') {
        await db.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ORDERS SET status = "PICKING" WHERE id = ?', [order.id]);
      }

      return { order, items };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore lettura ordine' });
    }
  });

  // POST /api/outbound/zebra/pick - Zebra confirms a pick step
  fastify.post('/zebra/pick', async (request, reply) => {
    const { order_id, item_id, pin, pallet_code, qty_picked } = request.body;
    try {
      // Verifica item
      const [items] = await db.query(`
        SELECT i.*, pal.id as resolved_pallet_id, pal.location, loc.pin as loc_pin, pr.name as product_name
        FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS i
        LEFT JOIN PALLETS pal ON pal.id = COALESCE(
          i.pallet_id,
          (SELECT p2.id FROM PALLETS p2 
           WHERE p2.pallet_code = i.pallet_code 
             AND p2.product_id = i.product_id 
             AND (i.batch IS NULL OR p2.batch = i.batch)
             AND p2.status != 'SHIPPED'
           ORDER BY p2.id ASC LIMIT 1)
        )
        LEFT JOIN LOCATIONS loc ON pal.location = loc.barcode
        JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON i.product_id = pr.id
        WHERE i.id = ? AND i.order_id = ?
      `, [item_id, order_id]);

      if (items.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Riga ordine non trovata' });
      const item = items[0];

      // Verifica Barcode Paletta
      if (item.pallet_code.toLowerCase() !== pallet_code.toLowerCase()) {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Codice Paletta errato (Lotto non corrispondente)' });
      }

      // Aggiorna Riga Ordine (e fissa pallet_id se non era già valorizzato)
      await db.query(
        'UPDATE OUTBOUND_ITEMS SET quantity_picked = ?, pallet_id = COALESCE(pallet_id, ?), status = "PICKED", picked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?',
        [qty_picked, item.resolved_pallet_id || null, item_id]
      );

      // Log Audit Zebra
      const operator = request.user?.username || 'ZEBRA';
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        [
          'OUTBOUND_PICK',
          `Prelevati ${qty_picked} di ${item.product_name} dalla paletta ${item.pallet_code}`,
          'ZEBRA',
          operator
        ]
      );

      // Verifica ordine e primo pezzo prelevato
      const [order] = await db.query('SELECT order_code, start_picking_at FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS WHERE id = ?', [order_id]);
      const orderCode = order[0]?.order_code || `ID-${order_id}`;
      if (order.length > 0 && !order[0].start_picking_at) {
        await db.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ORDERS SET start_picking_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, picking_operator = ? WHERE id = ?', [operator, order_id]);
      }

      // Se la paletta (o la specifica riga paletta) va a 0 scorta residua a scaffale dopo il prelievo,
      // spostiamo la sua posizione in ZONA CARICO (DDT) liberando subito il posto sullo scaffale,
      // senza però scaricarla dall'inventario finché il backoffice non conferma la spedizione.
      if (item.resolved_pallet_id) {
        const [[remRow]] = await db.query(`
          SELECT pal.id, pal.pallet_code, pal.location,
                 (pal.quantity - COALESCE((
                   SELECT SUM(
                     CASE 
                       WHEN oi.requested_uom IN ('Bancali', 'Palette') THEN 
                         CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.boxes_per_pallet, 1) > 1 AND pal.quantity >= 2 * (COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) * p.boxes_per_pallet)
                              THEN (COALESCE(NULLIF(oi.quantity_picked, 0), oi.quantity_required) * COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) * p.boxes_per_pallet)
                              ELSE pal.quantity 
                         END
                       WHEN oi.requested_uom IN ('Scatole', 'Cartoni') THEN (COALESCE(NULLIF(oi.quantity_picked, 0), oi.quantity_required) * COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1))
                       ELSE COALESCE(NULLIF(oi.quantity_picked, 0), oi.quantity_required)
                     END
                   )
                   FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS oi
                   JOIN OUTBOUND_ORDERS oo ON oi.order_id = oo.id
                   WHERE (
                     (oi.pallet_id IS NOT NULL AND oi.pallet_id = pal.id)
                     OR (oi.pallet_id IS NULL AND oi.pallet_code = pal.pallet_code AND oi.product_id = pal.product_id AND (oi.batch IS NULL OR oi.batch = pal.batch))
                   )
                   AND oi.status = 'PICKED'
                   AND oo.status != 'SHIPPED'
                 ), 0)) as remaining_on_shelf
          FROM PALLETS pal
          JOIN PRODUCTS p ON pal.product_id = p.id
          WHERE pal.id = ?
        `, [item.resolved_pallet_id]);

        if (remRow && parseFloat(remRow.remaining_on_shelf) <= 0) {
          const loadingLocation = `ZONA CARICO (${orderCode})`;
          if (remRow.location !== loadingLocation) {
            const prevLoc = remRow.location || 'NESSUNA';
            await db.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET location = ? WHERE id = ?', [loadingLocation, remRow.id]);
            await db.query(
              'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
              [
                'STOW',
                `Paletta ${remRow.pallet_code} spostata da ${prevLoc} a ${loadingLocation} (scaffale liberato in picking)`,
                'ZEBRA',
                operator
              ]
            );
            if (fastify.io) fastify.io.emit('stow_updated');
          }
        }
      }

      // Controllo se l'intero ordine è pronto
      const [all_items] = await db.query('SELECT status FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS WHERE order_id = ?', [order_id]);
      const allPicked = all_items.every(i => i.status === 'PICKED');
      
      if (allPicked) {
        await db.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ORDERS SET status = "READY", end_picking_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?', [order_id]);
      }

      if (fastify.io) fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');

      return { success: true, all_picked: allPicked };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore registrazione prelievo' });
    }
  });

  // POST /api/outbound/:code/confirm - Finalize order from PC
  fastify.post('/:code/confirm', async (request, reply) => {
    const { code } = request.params;
    const connection = await db.getConnection();
    
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();

      const [orders] = await connection.query('SELECT * FROM OUTBOUND_ORDERS WHERE order_code = ?', [code]);
      if (orders.length === 0) throw new Error('Ordine non trovato');
      const order = orders[0];

      if (order.status === 'SHIPPED') {
        throw new Error('L\'ordine è già stato confermato e spedito.');
      }

      const [items] = await connection.query('SELECT * FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS WHERE order_id = ?', [order.id]);

      for (const item of items) {
        // Usa quantity_picked se > 0 (Zebra usato), altrimenti assumi che dal PC si forzi quantity_required
        const actualPicked = parseFloat(item.quantity_picked) > 0 ? parseFloat(item.quantity_picked) : parseFloat(item.quantity_required);

        // Scarica la giacenza esattamente dalla riga PALLETS del lotto selezionato
        let palletsQuery = `
          SELECT pal.*, COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) as effective_units_per_box, COALESCE(p.boxes_per_pallet, 1) as effective_boxes_per_pallet
          FROM PALLETS pal
          JOIN PRODUCTS p ON pal.product_id = p.id
          WHERE 
        `;
        let palletsParams = [];
        if (item.pallet_id) {
          palletsQuery += `pal.id = ?`;
          palletsParams = [item.pallet_id];
        } else if (item.batch) {
          palletsQuery += `pal.pallet_code = ? AND pal.product_id = ? AND TRIM(pal.batch) = TRIM(?) AND pal.status != 'SHIPPED' ORDER BY pal.id ASC LIMIT 1`;
          palletsParams = [item.pallet_code, item.product_id, item.batch];
        } else {
          palletsQuery += `pal.pallet_code = ? AND pal.product_id = ? AND pal.status != 'SHIPPED' ORDER BY pal.id ASC LIMIT 1`;
          palletsParams = [item.pallet_code, item.product_id];
        }

        const [pallets] = await connection.query(palletsQuery, palletsParams);
        if (pallets.length > 0) {
          const pallet = pallets[0];
          const upb = parseFloat(pallet.effective_units_per_box) || 1;
          const bpp = parseFloat(pallet.effective_boxes_per_pallet) || 1;
          const unitsPerPallet = upb * bpp;
          let newQty;

          if ((item.requested_uom === 'Bancali' || item.requested_uom === 'Palette') && actualPicked >= 1) {
            if (unitsPerPallet > 1 && parseFloat(pallet.quantity) > actualPicked * unitsPerPallet) {
              newQty = parseFloat(pallet.quantity) - (actualPicked * unitsPerPallet);
            } else {
              newQty = 0;
            }
          } else {
            let deduction = actualPicked;
            if (item.requested_uom === 'Scatole' || item.requested_uom === 'Cartoni') {
              deduction *= upb;
            }
            newQty = parseFloat(pallet.quantity) - deduction;
          }
          
          if (newQty <= 0) {
            newQty = 0;
            // Paletta esaurita, svuota la posizione, marca SHIPPED e registra shipped_at
            await connection.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET quantity = 0, status = "SHIPPED", location = NULL, shipped_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?', [pallet.id]);
          } else {
            // Paletta parziale
            await connection.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET quantity = ? WHERE id = ?', [newQty, pallet.id]);
          }

          if (!parseFloat(item.quantity_picked) || parseFloat(item.quantity_picked) <= 0) {
            await connection.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ITEMS SET quantity_picked = ?, status = "PICKED" WHERE id = ?', [actualPicked, item.id]);
          }

          // Audit Log
          await connection.query(
            'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
            [
              'OUTBOUND_PICK', 
              `Prelevato ${actualPicked} ${item.requested_uom || ''} da paletta ${pallet.pallet_code}${pallet.batch ? ` (Lotto: ${pallet.batch})` : ''} (rimanenza: ${newQty})`,
              'BACKOFFICE',
              request.user?.username || 'SYSTEM'
            ]
          );
        }
      }

      if (!order.start_picking_at) {
        await connection.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ORDERS SET status = "SHIPPED", shipped_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, start_picking_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, end_picking_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, picking_operator = ?, shipped_by = ? WHERE id = ?', [request.user?.username || 'SYSTEM', request.user?.username || 'SYSTEM', order.id]);
        await connection.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ITEMS SET picked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE order_id = ? AND picked_at IS NULL', [order.id]);
      } else {
        await connection.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ORDERS SET status = "SHIPPED", shipped_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, shipped_by = ? WHERE id = ?', [request.user?.username || 'SYSTEM', order.id]);
      }

      await connection.commit();
      connection.release();
      
      if (fastify.io) {
        fastify.io.emit('stow_updated');
        fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');
      }
      
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      if (connection) {
        await connection.rollback();
        connection.release();
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: err.message || 'Errore durante la conferma spedizione' });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/outbound/search-pallets - Aiuto per PC per cercare palette disponibili
  fastify.get('/search-pallets', async (request, reply) => {
    const { customer_id, exclude_order_id, include_pending } = request.query;
    try {
      let excludeCondition = '';
      if (exclude_order_id && exclude_order_id !== 'undefined') {
        excludeCondition = `AND oo.id != ${db.escape(exclude_order_id)}`;
      }
      
      const statusCondition = include_pending === 'true' ? "IN ('STOCKED', 'PENDING')" : "= 'STOCKED'";

      const [pallets] = await db.query(`
        SELECT pal.*, l.zone, l.col, l.pos, p.name as product_name, p.sku as product_sku, COALESCE(pal.pallet_uom, p.uom) as uom, COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) as units_per_box, COALESCE(p.boxes_per_pallet, 1) as boxes_per_pallet,
               (pal.quantity - COALESCE((
                 SELECT SUM(
                   CASE 
                     WHEN oi.requested_uom IN ('Bancali', 'Palette') THEN 
                       CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.boxes_per_pallet, 1) > 1 AND pal.quantity >= 2 * (COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) * p.boxes_per_pallet)
                            THEN (oi.quantity_required * COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1) * p.boxes_per_pallet)
                            ELSE pal.quantity 
                       END
                     WHEN oi.requested_uom IN ('Scatole', 'Cartoni') THEN (oi.quantity_required * COALESCE(pal.units_per_box, p.units_per_box, 1))
                     ELSE oi.quantity_required 
                   END
                 )
                 FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS oi
                 JOIN OUTBOUND_ORDERS oo ON oi.order_id = oo.id
                 WHERE (
                   (oi.pallet_id IS NOT NULL AND oi.pallet_id = pal.id)
                   OR (oi.pallet_id IS NULL AND oi.pallet_code = pal.pallet_code AND oi.product_id = pal.product_id AND (oi.batch IS NULL OR oi.batch = pal.batch))
                 )
                 AND oo.status != 'SHIPPED'
                 ${excludeCondition}
               ), 0)) as available_quantity
        FROM PALLETS pal
        JOIN PRODUCTS p ON pal.product_id = p.id
        LEFT JOIN LOCATIONS l ON pal.location = l.barcode
        WHERE pal.customer_id = ? AND pal.status ${statusCondition}
        HAVING available_quantity > 0
        ORDER BY pal.expiration_date ASC, pal.created_at ASC
      `, [customer_id]);
      
      return pallets;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore ricerca palette' });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/outbound/history/items
  fastify.get('/history/items', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT 
          i.id,
          o.order_code,
          o.shipped_at,
          o.created_by AS operator_prep,
          o.shipped_by AS operator_ship,
          c.business_name AS customer_name,
          p.name AS product_name,
          i.pallet_code,
          COALESCE(i.batch, pal.batch) AS batch,
          CASE WHEN i.quantity_picked > 0 THEN i.quantity_picked ELSE i.quantity_required END AS quantity_picked,
          COALESCE(i.requested_uom, pal.pallet_uom, p.uom) AS uom
        FROM OUTBOUND_ITEMS i
        JOIN OUTBOUND_ORDERS o ON i.order_id = o.id
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON o.customer_id = c.id
        JOIN PRODUCTS p ON i.product_id = p.id
        LEFT JOIN PALLETS pal ON pal.id = COALESCE(
          i.pallet_id,
          (SELECT p2.id FROM PALLETS p2 
           WHERE p2.pallet_code = i.pallet_code 
             AND p2.product_id = i.product_id 
             AND (i.batch IS NULL OR p2.batch = i.batch)
           ORDER BY p2.id ASC LIMIT 1)
        )
        WHERE o.status = 'SHIPPED'
        ORDER BY o.shipped_at DESC
      `);
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore nel recupero dello storico uscite' });
    }
  });

}
module.exports = outboundRoutes;
=== FILE: backend/src/routes/pallets.js ===
const db = require('../config/db');
const { drawLabel, generateLabelsPdfBuffer, sanitizeExpirationDate } = require('../utils/pdfLabels');

async function ensureLocationExists(connection, locStr, username) {
  if (!locStr) return;
  const cleanLoc = locStr.trim().toUpperCase().slice(0, 50);
  if (!cleanLoc) return;

  const [[existingLoc]] = await connection.query('SELECT id FROM LOCATIONS WHERE barcode = ?', [cleanLoc]);
  if (existingLoc) return;

  const [[existingFree]] = await connection.query('SELECT id FROM FREE_LOCATIONS WHERE UPPER(name) = ?', [cleanLoc]);
  if (existingFree) return;

  const parts = cleanLoc.split('-');
  const zone = (parts[0] || 'Z').slice(0, 10);
  const col = (parts[1] || '00').slice(0, 10);
  const pos = (parts[2] || '00').slice(0, 10);
  const finalPin = Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900).toString();

  await connection.query(
    'INSERT IGNORE INTO LOCATIONS (zone, col, pos, barcode, pin) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
    [zone, col, pos, cleanLoc, finalPin]
  );
  await connection.query(
    'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
    ['CREATE_LOC', `Creata posizione automatica ${cleanLoc}`, 'Gestionale', username || 'Sistema']
  );
}

// Generatore progressivo di codici paletta giornalieri (es. PAL-20261020-0001, 0002, ...)
async function createPalletCodeGenerator(connection, prefix = 'PAL') {
  const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, '');
  const basePrefix = `${prefix}-${dateStr}-`;
  const [rows] = await connection.query(
    'SELECT DISTINCT pallet_code FROM PALLETS WHERE pallet_code LIKE ?',
    [`${basePrefix}%`]
  );
  let maxSeq = 0;
  for (const r of rows) {
    const suffix = String(r.pallet_code || '').slice(basePrefix.length);
    if (/^\d+$/.test(suffix)) {
      const num = parseInt(suffix, 10);
      if (num > maxSeq) maxSeq = num;
    }
  }
  return () => {
    maxSeq += 1;
    return `${basePrefix}${String(maxSeq).padStart(4, '0')}`;
  };
}

async function palletRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  fastify.get('/', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.*, pr.name as product_name, COALESCE(p.pallet_uom, pr.uom) as uom, pr.notes as product_notes, c.business_name as customer_name, COALESCE(p.units_per_box, pr.units_per_box) as units_per_box,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) > 1 FROM PALLETS p2 WHERE p2.pallet_code = p.pallet_code) as is_mixed
        FROM PALLETS p
        JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON p.customer_id = c.id
        WHERE p.status != 'SHIPPED'
        ORDER BY p.created_at DESC
      `);
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante il recupero storico palette' });
    }
  });

  fastify.get('/inventory', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.*, pr.name as product_name, COALESCE(p.pallet_uom, pr.uom) as uom, pr.notes as product_notes, c.business_name as customer_name,
               l.zone, l.col, l.pos,
               (SELECT COUNT(*) > 1 FROM PALLETS p2 WHERE p2.pallet_code = p.pallet_code) as is_mixed
        FROM PALLETS p
        JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON p.customer_id = c.id
        LEFT JOIN LOCATIONS l ON p.location = l.barcode
        WHERE p.status != 'SHIPPED'
      `);
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante il recupero inventario' });
    }
  });

  fastify.post('/generate', async (request, reply) => {
    const { cart, printOptions } = request.body;
    
    if (!cart || !Array.isArray(cart) || cart.length === 0) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Carrello vuoto o formato non valido' });
    }

    const { paper_format = 'A4', print_mode = 'GRID', isMixed = false, noBarcode = false, freeLocation = '' } = printOptions || {};

    let connection = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();
      
      const generatedLabelsData = []; 
      const nextPalletCode = await createPalletCodeGenerator(connection, noBarcode ? 'NOBAR' : 'PAL');
      
      if (isMixed || noBarcode) {
        // Generate ONLY ONE pallet code for the whole cart
        const firstCartLoc = cart.map(i => (i.location || '').trim().toUpperCase()).find(Boolean) || '';
        const finalLocation = noBarcode ? freeLocation.trim().toUpperCase() : (firstCartLoc || null);
        const finalStatus = (noBarcode || finalLocation) ? 'STOCKED' : 'PENDING';

        if (finalLocation && !noBarcode) {
          await ensureLocationExists(connection, finalLocation, request.user.username);
        }

        const code = nextPalletCode();
        if (finalLocation && !noBarcode) {
          await connection.query(
            "UPDATE PALLETS SET pallet_code = ? WHERE location = ? AND status = 'STOCKED'",
            [code, finalLocation]
          );
        }
        
        for (const item of cart) {
          const { customer_id, product_id, quantity, batch, warehouse, notes, client_pallet_number, client_article_number, expiration_date, arrival_date } = item;
          const cleanExpDate = sanitizeExpirationDate(expiration_date);
          
          await connection.query(
            'INSERT INTO PALLETS (pallet_code, customer_id, product_id, quantity, units_per_box, pallet_uom, batch, warehouse, status, location, notes, client_pallet_number, client_article_number, expiration_date, created_at, flags) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, COALESCE(?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP), ?)',
            [code, customer_id, product_id, quantity, item.units_per_box || null, item.pallet_uom || null, batch || null, warehouse, finalStatus, finalLocation, notes || null, client_pallet_number || null, client_article_number || null, cleanExpDate, arrival_date ? new Date(arrival_date) : null, item.flags ? JSON.stringify(item.flags) : null]
          );
          
          if (item.units_per_box || item.boxes_per_pallet) {
            await connection.query(
              'UPDATE PRODUCTS SET units_per_box = ?, boxes_per_pallet = ? WHERE id = ?',
              [item.units_per_box || 1, item.boxes_per_pallet || 1, product_id]
            );
          }
        }
        
        await connection.query(
          "INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
          ['INBOUND', noBarcode ? `Carico libero in ${freeLocation} (${cart.length} art.)` : `Creata paletta frammentata ${code} (${cart.length} articoli)${finalLocation ? ' in ' + finalLocation : ''}`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
        );
        
        if (!noBarcode) {
          const [[customer]] = await connection.query('SELECT business_name FROM CUSTOMERS WHERE id = ?', [cart[0].customer_id]);
          generatedLabelsData.push({
            code,
            customer_name: customer.business_name,
            product_name: `PALETTA MISTA (${cart.length} Articoli)`,
            quantity: '-',
            batch: '-',
            warehouse: cart[0].warehouse,
            client_pallet_number: cart[0].client_pallet_number,
            client_article_number: '-',
            expiration_date: sanitizeExpirationDate(cart[0].expiration_date),
            location: finalLocation
          });
        }
      } else {
        // Generazione rigorosamente progressiva e in ordine di riga (1° riga -> 1° barcode, 2° riga -> 2° barcode, ecc.)
        // Se più righe condividono la stessa identica posizione, si frammentano nel barcode assegnato alla prima riga di quella posizione.
        const orderedSlots = [];
        const locSlotMap = new Map();

        for (const item of cart) {
          const { customer_id, product_id, quantity, batch, warehouse, num_pallets, notes, client_pallet_number, client_article_number, expiration_date, arrival_date } = item;
          const itemLoc = (item.location || '').trim().toUpperCase();
          const cleanExpDate = sanitizeExpirationDate(expiration_date);
          
          const [[product]] = await connection.query('SELECT name FROM PRODUCTS WHERE id = ?', [product_id]);
          const [[customer]] = await connection.query('SELECT business_name FROM CUSTOMERS WHERE id = ?', [customer_id]);

          if (itemLoc) {
            let slot = locSlotMap.get(itemLoc);
            if (!slot) {
              await ensureLocationExists(connection, itemLoc, request.user.username);
              const code = nextPalletCode();
              // Unifica eventuali palette già stivate nella medesima posizione sotto il nuovo codice progressivo
              await connection.query(
                "UPDATE PALLETS SET pallet_code = ? WHERE location = ? AND status = 'STOCKED'",
                [code, itemLoc]
              );
              slot = { type: 'LOC', loc: itemLoc, code, rows: [] };
              locSlotMap.set(itemLoc, slot);
              orderedSlots.push(slot);
            }

            const countPallets = Math.max(1, parseInt(num_pallets, 10) || 1);
            const totalQtyForRow = (parseFloat(quantity) || 0) * countPallets;

            await connection.query(
              'INSERT INTO PALLETS (pallet_code, customer_id, product_id, quantity, units_per_box, pallet_uom, batch, warehouse, status, location, notes, client_pallet_number, client_article_number, expiration_date, created_at, flags) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, COALESCE(?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP), ?)',
              [slot.code, customer_id, product_id, totalQtyForRow, item.units_per_box || null, item.pallet_uom || null, batch || null, warehouse, 'STOCKED', itemLoc, notes || null, client_pallet_number || null, client_article_number || null, cleanExpDate, arrival_date ? new Date(arrival_date) : null, item.flags ? JSON.stringify(item.flags) : null]
            );

            await connection.query(
              "INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
              ['INBOUND', `Creata/stivata paletta ${slot.code} in ${itemLoc} (${totalQtyForRow} q.tà)`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
            );

            slot.rows.push({
              customer_name: customer.business_name,
              product_name: product.name,
              quantity: totalQtyForRow,
              batch,
              warehouse,
              client_pallet_number,
              client_article_number,
              expiration_date: cleanExpDate
            });
          } else {
            const countPallets = Math.max(1, parseInt(num_pallets, 10) || 1);
            for (let i = 0; i < countPallets; i++) {
              const code = nextPalletCode();
              
              await connection.query(
                'INSERT INTO PALLETS (pallet_code, customer_id, product_id, quantity, units_per_box, pallet_uom, batch, warehouse, status, location, notes, client_pallet_number, client_article_number, expiration_date, created_at, flags) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, COALESCE(?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP), ?)',
                [code, customer_id, product_id, quantity, item.units_per_box || null, item.pallet_uom || null, batch || null, warehouse, 'PENDING', null, notes || null, client_pallet_number || null, client_article_number || null, cleanExpDate, arrival_date ? new Date(arrival_date) : null, item.flags ? JSON.stringify(item.flags) : null]
              );
              
              await connection.query(
                "INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                ['INBOUND', `Creata paletta ${code} (${quantity} q.tà)`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
              );
              
              orderedSlots.push({
                type: 'DIRECT',
                label: {
                  code,
                  customer_name: customer.business_name,
                  product_name: product.name,
                  quantity,
                  batch,
                  warehouse,
                  client_pallet_number,
                  client_article_number,
                  expiration_date: cleanExpDate,
                  location: null
                }
              });
            }
          }

          // AGGIORNA ANAGRAFICA PRODOTTO CON I NUOVI MOLTIPLICATORI INSERITI IN INBOUND
          if (item.units_per_box || item.boxes_per_pallet) {
            await connection.query(
              'UPDATE PRODUCTS SET units_per_box = ?, boxes_per_pallet = ? WHERE id = ?',
              [item.units_per_box || 1, item.boxes_per_pallet || 1, product_id]
            );
          }
        }

        // Compila generatedLabelsData rispettando rigorosamente l'ordine di riga (orderedSlots)
        for (const slot of orderedSlots) {
          if (slot.type === 'DIRECT') {
            generatedLabelsData.push(slot.label);
          } else if (slot.type === 'LOC' && slot.rows.length > 0) {
            if (slot.rows.length === 1) {
              const r = slot.rows[0];
              generatedLabelsData.push({
                code: slot.code,
                customer_name: r.customer_name,
                product_name: r.product_name,
                quantity: r.quantity,
                batch: r.batch,
                warehouse: r.warehouse,
                client_pallet_number: r.client_pallet_number,
                client_article_number: r.client_article_number,
                expiration_date: r.expiration_date,
                location: slot.loc
              });
            } else {
              const first = slot.rows[0];
              const uniqueProdNames = [...new Set(slot.rows.map(r => r.product_name))];
              const uniqueBatches = [...new Set(slot.rows.map(r => r.batch).filter(Boolean))];
              const uniqueArticles = [...new Set(slot.rows.map(r => r.client_article_number).filter(Boolean))];

              const labelProdName = uniqueProdNames.length === 1
                ? `${uniqueProdNames[0]} (${slot.rows.length} LOTTI)`
                : `PALETTA MISTA (${slot.rows.length} Articoli)`;

              generatedLabelsData.push({
                code: slot.code,
                customer_name: first.customer_name,
                product_name: labelProdName,
                quantity: '-',
                batch: uniqueBatches.length > 0 ? uniqueBatches.join(', ') : '-',
                warehouse: first.warehouse,
                client_pallet_number: first.client_pallet_number,
                client_article_number: uniqueArticles.length > 0 ? uniqueArticles.join(', ') : '-',
                expiration_date: first.expiration_date,
                location: slot.loc
              });
            }
          }
        }
      }
      await connection.commit();
      connection.release();
      connection = null;

      if (noBarcode) {
        if (fastify.io) fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');
        return reply.send({ success: true, message: 'Stock libero salvato senza barcode' });
      }

      const pdfBuffer = await generateLabelsPdfBuffer(generatedLabelsData, { paper_format, print_mode });

      if (fastify.io) {
        fastify.io.emit('stow_updated');
        fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');
      }

      reply.header('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
      reply.header('Content-Disposition', 'inline; filename="etichette.pdf"');
      return reply.send(pdfBuffer);
    } catch (err) {
      if (connection) {
        await connection.rollback();
        connection.release();
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: err.message || 'Errore durante la generazione' });
    }
  });

  fastify.get('/:code', async (request, reply) => {
    const { code } = request.params;
    try {
      const [allRows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.*, pr.name as product_name, COALESCE(p.pallet_uom, pr.uom) as uom,
               COALESCE(p.units_per_box, pr.units_per_box, 1) as units_per_box,
               c.business_name as customer_name
        FROM PALLETS p
        JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON p.customer_id = c.id
        WHERE p.pallet_code = ?
      `, [code]);
      
      if (allRows.length === 0) {
        return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Paletta non trovata' });
      }

      const activeRows = allRows.filter(r => r.status !== 'SHIPPED' && parseFloat(r.quantity) > 0);
      const rows = activeRows.length > 0 ? activeRows : allRows;

      if (rows.length > 1) {
        // Paletta mista
        const mixedPallet = {
          ...rows[0],
          product_name: `PALETTA MISTA (${rows.length} Articoli)`,
          isMixed: true,
          items: rows
        };
        return mixedPallet;
      }
      
      return rows[0];
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore lettura paletta' });
    }
  });

  fastify.get('/:code/print', async (request, reply) => {
    const { code } = request.params;
    try {
      const [allRows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.*, pr.name as product_name, COALESCE(p.pallet_uom, pr.uom) as uom, c.business_name as customer_name
        FROM PALLETS p
        JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON p.customer_id = c.id
        WHERE p.pallet_code = ?
        ORDER BY p.id ASC
      `, [code]);

      if (allRows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Paletta non trovata' });
      const activeRows = allRows.filter(r => r.status !== 'SHIPPED' && parseFloat(r.quantity) > 0);
      const rows = activeRows.length > 0 ? activeRows : allRows;
      const pallet = rows[0];

      const paper_format = request.query.format || 'THERMAL';
      const print_mode = request.query.mode || 'GRID';
      const copies = parseInt(request.query.copies, 10) || 1;

      const uniqueProdNames = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.product_name))];
      const uniqueBatches = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.batch).filter(Boolean))];
      const uniqueArticles = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.client_article_number).filter(Boolean))];

      const lbl = {
        code: pallet.pallet_code,
        customer_name: pallet.customer_name,
        product_name: rows.length > 1
          ? (uniqueProdNames.length === 1 ? `${uniqueProdNames[0]} (${rows.length} LOTTI)` : `PALETTA MISTA (${rows.length} Articoli)`)
          : pallet.product_name,
        quantity: rows.length > 1 ? '-' : pallet.quantity,
        uom: pallet.uom,
        batch: rows.length > 1 ? (uniqueBatches.join(', ') || '-') : pallet.batch,
        warehouse: pallet.warehouse,
        client_pallet_number: pallet.client_pallet_number,
        client_article_number: rows.length > 1 ? (uniqueArticles.join(', ') || '-') : pallet.client_article_number,
        expiration_date: pallet.expiration_date,
        flags: pallet.flags,
        location: pallet.location || null
      };

      const generatedLabelsData = Array(copies).fill(lbl);
      const pdfBuffer = await generateLabelsPdfBuffer(generatedLabelsData, { paper_format, print_mode });

      reply.header('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
      reply.header('Content-Disposition', `inline; filename="${code}.pdf"`);
      return reply.send(pdfBuffer);
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore stampa' });
    }
  });

  fastify.post('/print-bulk', async (request, reply) => {
    const { ids, paper_format = 'A4', print_mode = 'GRID', copies = 1 } = request.body;
    if (!ids || !Array.isArray(ids) || ids.length === 0) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Nessun ID fornito' });
    }

    try {
      const [codeRows] = await db.query(`SELECT pallet_code FROM PALLETS WHERE id IN (?) GROUP BY pallet_code ORDER BY MIN(id) ASC`, [ids]);
      const codes = codeRows.map(r => r.pallet_code);

      const generatedLabelsData = [];
      
      for (const code of codes) {
        const [allRows] = await db.query(`
          SELECT p.*, pr.name as product_name, COALESCE(p.pallet_uom, pr.uom) as uom, c.business_name as customer_name
          FROM PALLETS p
          JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
          JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON p.customer_id = c.id
          WHERE p.pallet_code = ?
          ORDER BY p.id ASC
        `, [code]);
        
        if (allRows.length === 0) continue;
        
        const activeRows = allRows.filter(r => r.status !== 'SHIPPED' && parseFloat(r.quantity) > 0);
        const rows = activeRows.length > 0 ? activeRows : allRows;
        const pallet = rows[0];
        const uniqueProdNames = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.product_name))];
        const uniqueBatches = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.batch).filter(Boolean))];
        const uniqueArticles = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.client_article_number).filter(Boolean))];
        
        generatedLabelsData.push({
          code: pallet.pallet_code,
          customer_name: pallet.customer_name,
          product_name: rows.length > 1
            ? (uniqueProdNames.length === 1 ? `${uniqueProdNames[0]} (${rows.length} LOTTI)` : `PALETTA MISTA (${rows.length} Articoli)`)
            : pallet.product_name,
          quantity: rows.length > 1 ? '-' : pallet.quantity,
          uom: pallet.uom,
          batch: rows.length > 1 ? (uniqueBatches.join(', ') || '-') : pallet.batch,
          warehouse: pallet.warehouse,
          client_pallet_number: pallet.client_pallet_number,
          client_article_number: rows.length > 1 ? (uniqueArticles.join(', ') || '-') : pallet.client_article_number,
          expiration_date: pallet.expiration_date,
          flags: pallet.flags,
          location: pallet.location || null
        });
      }

      const finalLabels = [];
      for (const lbl of generatedLabelsData) {
        for (let i = 0; i < copies; i++) {
          finalLabels.push(lbl);
        }
      }

      const pdfBuffer = await generateLabelsPdfBuffer(finalLabels, { paper_format, print_mode });

      reply.header('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
      reply.header('Content-Disposition', 'inline; filename="etichette_bulk.pdf"');
      return reply.send(pdfBuffer);
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore stampa bulk' });
    }
  });

  fastify.put('/:code/quantity', async (request, reply) => {
    const { code } = request.params;
    const { quantity, pallet_id } = request.body;

    if (quantity === undefined || quantity < 0) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Quantità non valida' });
    }

    try {
      let oldPallet;
      if (pallet_id) {
        [[oldPallet]] = await db.query('SELECT quantity FROM PALLETS WHERE id = ?', [pallet_id]);
        if (!oldPallet) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Paletta non trovata' });
        await db.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET quantity = ? WHERE id = ?', [quantity, pallet_id]);
      } else {
        [[oldPallet]] = await db.query('SELECT quantity FROM PALLETS WHERE pallet_code = ?', [code]);
        if (!oldPallet) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Paletta non trovata' });
        await db.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET quantity = ? WHERE pallet_code = ?', [quantity, code]);
      }

      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['ADJUST_QTY', `Modificata q.tà paletta ${code}: da ${oldPallet.quantity} a ${quantity}`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );

      if (fastify.io) fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');

      return { success: true, new_quantity: quantity };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante la modifica della quantità' });
    }
  });


  fastify.put('/:code', async (request, reply) => {
    const { code } = request.params;
    const { notes, client_pallet_number, client_article_number, expiration_date } = request.body;
    try {
      await db.query(
        'UPDATE PALLETS SET notes = ?, client_pallet_number = ?, client_article_number = ?, expiration_date = ? WHERE pallet_code = ?',
        [notes || null, client_pallet_number || null, client_article_number || null, sanitizeExpirationDate(expiration_date), code]
      );
      
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['UPDATE', `Aggiornati metadati paletta ${code}`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );
      
      if (fastify.io) fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');
      
      return { success: true };
    } catch(err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore salvataggio modifiche' });
    }
  });

  fastify.delete('/:code', async (request, reply) => {
    if (request.user.role !== 'developer' && request.user.role !== 'backoffice') {
      return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Solo per developer o backoffice' });
    }
    const { code } = request.params;
    try {
      await db.query('DELETE FROM PALLETS WHERE pallet_code = ?', [code]);
      
      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['DELETE', `Eliminata fisicamente paletta ${code}`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );

      if (fastify.io) {
        fastify.io.emit('stow_updated');
        fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');
      }
      return { success: true };
    } catch(err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore eliminazione paletta' });
    }
  });


  // Unisci paletta a un'altra (Frammenta)
  fastify.post('/merge', async (request, reply) => {
    const { source_code, target_code } = request.body;
    if (!source_code || !target_code) return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Codici mancanti' });
    if (source_code === target_code) return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'I codici sono identici' });
    
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT id FROM PALLETS WHERE pallet_code = ?', [target_code]);
      if (rows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Paletta di destinazione non trovata' });
      
      await db.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET pallet_code = ? WHERE pallet_code = ?', [target_code, source_code]);
      
      await db.query(
          "INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
          ['MERGE', `Paletta ${source_code} frammentata e unita a ${target_code}`, 'Gestionale', request.user ? request.user.username : 'Sistema']
        );
      
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore unione palette' });
    }
  });
  
  fastify.post('/stow', async (request, reply) => {
    const { pallet_code, location, pin, source = 'Zebra', force = false } = request.body;
    
    fastify.log.info(`[STOW] Received stow request: pallet=${pallet_code}, location=${location}, force=${force}`);

    if (!pallet_code || !location) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Pallet Code e Location richiesti' });
    }

    try {
      if (!force) {
        const [occupied] = await db.query(
          "SELECT id, pallet_code FROM PALLETS WHERE location = ? AND status = 'STOCKED' AND quantity > 0 AND pallet_code != ?", 
          [location, pallet_code]
        );
        
        if (occupied.length > 0) {
          fastify.log.info(`[STOW] Location occupied! Returning 409`);
          return reply.code(409).send({ 
            error: `La posizione ${location} è già occupata dalla paletta ${occupied[0].pallet_code}!`,
            occupying_pallet: occupied[0].pallet_code 
          });
        }
      } else {
        fastify.log.info(`[STOW] Force is true! Bypassing occupied check.`);
      }

      const [[loc]] = await db.query('SELECT * FROM LOCATIONS WHERE barcode = ?', [location]);
      
      if (loc) {
        if (pin && loc.pin !== pin) {
          return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'PIN di sicurezza ERRATO' });
        }
      } else {
        const parts = location.split('-');
        const zone = parts[0] || 'Z';
        const col = parts[1] || '00';
        const pos = parts[2] || '00';
        const finalPin = pin || Math.floor(10 + Math.random() * 90).toString(); 
        
        await db.query(
          'INSERT INTO LOCATIONS (zone, col, pos, barcode, pin) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
          [zone, col, pos, location, finalPin]
        );
        await db.query(
          'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
          ['CREATE_LOC', `Creata nuova posizione ${location}`, source || 'Zebra', request.user.username]
        );
      }

      const [result] = await db.query(
        'UPDATE PALLETS SET location = ?, status = ? WHERE pallet_code = ?',
        [location, 'STOCKED', pallet_code]
      );
      
      if (result.affectedRows === 0) {
        return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Paletta non trovata' });
      }

      await db.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['STOW', `Paletta ${pallet_code} stivata in ${location}`, source || 'Zebra', request.user.username]
      );

      if (fastify.io) {
        fastify.io.emit('stow_updated');
        fastify.io.emit('dashboard_update');
      }

      return { success: true, message: `Paletta stivata in ${location}` };
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante lo stivaggio' });
    }
  });
  
  fastify.get('/:code/history', async (request, reply) => {
    const { code } = request.params;
    try {
      const [allRows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.*, pr.name as product_name, pr.notes as product_notes, c.business_name as customer_name
        FROM PALLETS p
        JOIN PRODUCTS pr ON p.product_id = pr.id
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON p.customer_id = c.id
        WHERE p.pallet_code = ?
        ORDER BY p.id ASC
      `, [code]);
      
      if (allRows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Paletta non trovata' });
      const activeRows = allRows.filter(r => r.status !== 'SHIPPED' && parseFloat(r.quantity) > 0);
      const rows = activeRows.length > 0 ? activeRows : allRows;
      const uniqueProdNames = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.product_name))];
      const uniqueBatches = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.batch).filter(Boolean))];

      const pallet = rows.length > 1
        ? {
            ...rows[0],
            product_name: uniqueProdNames.length === 1
              ? `${uniqueProdNames[0]} (${rows.length} LOTTI)`
              : `PALETTA MISTA (${rows.length} Articoli)`,
            batch: uniqueBatches.join(', ') || rows[0].batch,
            isMixed: true,
            items: rows
          }
        : rows[0];
      
      const [history] = await db.query(`
        SELECT * FROM AUDIT_LOGS
        WHERE details LIKE ?
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
      `, [`%${code}%`]);
      
      return { pallet, history };
    } catch(err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore server' });
    }
  });
}

module.exports = palletRoutes;
=== FILE: backend/src/routes/products.js ===
const db = require('../config/db');

async function productRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  // Solo backoffice e developer
  fastify.addHook('preHandler', async (request, reply) => {
    if (request.user.role !== 'developer' && request.user.role !== 'backoffice') {
      return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Accesso negato.' });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/products
  fastify.get('/', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query(`
        SELECT p.*, c.business_name as customer_name 
        FROM PRODUCTS p 
        JOIN CUSTOMERS c ON p.customer_id = c.id 
        ORDER BY p.name ASC
      `);
      return rows;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore durante il recupero dei prodotti' });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/products/flags
  fastify.get('/flags', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [prodRows] = await db.query(`SELECT flags FROM PRODUCTS WHERE flags IS NOT NULL AND flags != '[]'`);
      const [palRows] = await db.query(`SELECT flags FROM PALLETS WHERE flags IS NOT NULL AND flags != '[]'`);
      
      const flagsSet = new Set();
      
      const processFlags = (rows) => {
        rows.forEach(r => {
          if (r.flags) {
            try {
              const parsed = typeof r.flags === 'string' ? JSON.parse(r.flags) : r.flags;
              if (Array.isArray(parsed)) parsed.forEach(f => flagsSet.add(f));
            } catch(e) {}
          }
        });
      };
      
      processFlags(prodRows);
      processFlags(palRows);
      
      return Array.from(flagsSet);
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore recupero flags' });
    }
  });

  // POST /api/products
  fastify.post('/', async (request, reply) => {
    const { sku, name, uom, customer_id, notes, units_per_box, boxes_per_pallet, default_article_number, flags } = request.body;
    
    const cleanArticle = default_article_number ? String(default_article_number).trim() : '';
    const rawSku = sku ? String(sku).trim() : '';
    const cleanSku = (cleanArticle && (!rawSku || rawSku.startsWith('PROD-'))) ? cleanArticle : rawSku;
    const cleanName = name ? String(name).trim() : '';

    if (!cleanSku || !cleanName || !customer_id) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'SKU, Nome e Cliente sono obbligatori' });
    }

    const finalDefaultArticle = cleanArticle || (!cleanSku.startsWith('PROD-') ? cleanSku : null);
    const flagsJson = flags ? JSON.stringify(flags) : null;

    const connection = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();

      // Verifica se esiste già un prodotto con questo SKU o con lo stesso nome (ed eventuale vecchio SKU PROD-XXXX)
      const [bySkuRows] = await connection.query('SELECT * FROM PRODUCTS WHERE sku = ?', [cleanSku]);
      const [byNameRows] = await connection.query(
        'SELECT * FROM PRODUCTS WHERE customer_id = ? AND (LOWER(TRIM(name)) = LOWER(?) OR (default_article_number IS NOT NULL AND default_article_number != "" AND LOWER(TRIM(default_article_number)) = LOWER(?)))',
        [customer_id, cleanName, cleanSku]
      );

      if (bySkuRows.length > 0) {
        const target = bySkuRows[0];
        // Elimina e unisci eventuali vecchi duplicati con SKU generato PROD-XXXX per lo stesso prodotto
        for (const dup of byNameRows) {
          if (dup.id !== target.id && dup.sku && dup.sku.startsWith('PROD-')) {
            await connection.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET product_id = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [target.id, dup.id]);
            await connection.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS SET product_id = ?, sku = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [target.id, cleanSku, dup.id]);
            await connection.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ITEMS SET product_id = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [target.id, dup.id]);
            await connection.query('DELETE FROM PRODUCTS WHERE id = ?', [dup.id]);
          }
        }
        await connection.query(
          'UPDATE PRODUCTS SET name = ?, uom = COALESCE(?, uom), units_per_box = COALESCE(?, units_per_box), boxes_per_pallet = COALESCE(?, boxes_per_pallet), default_article_number = COALESCE(?, default_article_number), flags = COALESCE(?, flags) WHERE id = ?',
          [cleanName, uom || null, units_per_box || null, boxes_per_pallet || null, finalDefaultArticle, flagsJson, target.id]
        );
        await connection.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS SET sku = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [cleanSku, target.id]);
        await connection.commit();
        return reply.code(200).send({ success: true, id: target.id, sku: cleanSku });
      }

      if (byNameRows.length > 0 && (!cleanSku.startsWith('PROD-') || byNameRows.some(r => r.sku && r.sku.startsWith('PROD-')))) {
        const target = byNameRows[0];
        const newSkuForTarget = !cleanSku.startsWith('PROD-') ? cleanSku : target.sku;
        for (const dup of byNameRows) {
          if (dup.id !== target.id && dup.sku && dup.sku.startsWith('PROD-')) {
            await connection.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET product_id = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [target.id, dup.id]);
            await connection.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS SET product_id = ?, sku = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [target.id, newSkuForTarget, dup.id]);
            await connection.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ITEMS SET product_id = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [target.id, dup.id]);
            await connection.query('DELETE FROM PRODUCTS WHERE id = ?', [dup.id]);
          }
        }
        await connection.query(
          'UPDATE PRODUCTS SET sku = ?, name = ?, uom = COALESCE(?, uom), units_per_box = COALESCE(?, units_per_box), boxes_per_pallet = COALESCE(?, boxes_per_pallet), default_article_number = COALESCE(?, default_article_number), flags = COALESCE(?, flags) WHERE id = ?',
          [newSkuForTarget, cleanName, uom || null, units_per_box || null, boxes_per_pallet || null, finalDefaultArticle, flagsJson, target.id]
        );
        await connection.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS SET sku = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [newSkuForTarget, target.id]);
        await connection.commit();
        return reply.code(200).send({ success: true, id: target.id, sku: newSkuForTarget });
      }

      const [result] = await connection.query(
        'INSERT INTO PRODUCTS (sku, name, uom, customer_id, notes, units_per_box, boxes_per_pallet, default_article_number, flags) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
        [cleanSku, cleanName, uom || 'Scatole', customer_id, notes || null, units_per_box || 1, boxes_per_pallet || 1, finalDefaultArticle, flagsJson]
      );
      await connection.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['CREATE_PRODUCT', `Creato prodotto ${cleanName} (${cleanSku})`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );
      await connection.commit();
      return reply.code(201).send({ success: true, id: result.insertId, sku: cleanSku });
    } catch (err) {
      await connection.rollback();
      if (err.code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Codice Articolo (SKU) già esistente' });
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore interno' });
    } finally {
      connection.release();
    }
  });

  // PUT /api/products/:id
  fastify.put('/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    const { sku, name, uom, customer_id, notes, units_per_box, boxes_per_pallet, default_article_number, flags, updateExistingPallets } = request.body;
    const targetId = parseInt(request.params.id, 10);
    
    const cleanArticle = default_article_number ? String(default_article_number).trim() : '';
    const rawSku = sku ? String(sku).trim() : '';
    const cleanSku = (cleanArticle && (!rawSku || rawSku.startsWith('PROD-'))) ? cleanArticle : rawSku;
    const cleanName = name ? String(name).trim() : '';

    if (!cleanSku || !cleanName || !customer_id) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'SKU, Nome e Cliente sono obbligatori' });
    }

    const finalDefaultArticle = cleanArticle || (!cleanSku.startsWith('PROD-') ? cleanSku : null);
    const flagsJson = flags ? JSON.stringify(flags) : null;

    const connection = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await connection.beginTransaction();

      // Se esiste un altro prodotto con lo stesso SKU o stesso nome e SKU generato PROD-%, uniscilo ed eliminalo
      const [conflictRows] = await connection.query(
        'SELECT * FROM PRODUCTS WHERE id != ? AND (sku = ? OR (customer_id = ? AND LOWER(TRIM(name)) = LOWER(?) AND sku LIKE "PROD-%"))',
        [targetId, cleanSku, customer_id, cleanName]
      );
      for (const dup of conflictRows) {
        await connection.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET product_id = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [targetId, dup.id]);
        await connection.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS SET product_id = ?, sku = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [targetId, cleanSku, dup.id]);
        await connection.query('UPDATE OUTBOUND_ITEMS SET product_id = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [targetId, dup.id]);
        await connection.query('DELETE FROM PRODUCTS WHERE id = ?', [dup.id]);
      }

      await connection.query(
        'UPDATE PRODUCTS SET sku = ?, name = ?, uom = ?, customer_id = ?, notes = ?, units_per_box = ?, boxes_per_pallet = ?, default_article_number = ?, flags = ? WHERE id = ?',
        [cleanSku, cleanName, uom || 'Scatole', customer_id, notes || null, units_per_box || 1, boxes_per_pallet || 1, finalDefaultArticle, flagsJson, targetId]
      );

      await connection.query('UPDATE INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS SET sku = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [cleanSku, targetId]);
      
      if (updateExistingPallets) {
        await connection.query('UPDATE PALLETS SET flags = ? WHERE product_id = ?', [flagsJson, targetId]);
      }

      await connection.query(
        'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['UPDATE_PRODUCT', `Aggiornato prodotto ${cleanName} (${cleanSku})`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
      );
      
      await connection.commit();
      return { success: true, sku: cleanSku };
    } catch (err) {
      await connection.rollback();
      if (err.code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Codice Articolo (SKU) già esistente' });
      }
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore interno' });
    } finally {
      connection.release();
    }
  });

  fastify.delete('/:id', async (request, reply) => {
    if (request.user.role !== 'developer' && request.user.role !== 'backoffice') {
      return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Solo developer e backoffice possono eliminare anagrafiche' });
    }
    try {
      const [[product]] = await db.query('SELECT name FROM PRODUCTS WHERE id = ?', [request.params.id]);
      await db.query('DELETE FROM PRODUCTS WHERE id = ?', [request.params.id]);
      if (product) {
        await db.query(
          'INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOGS (action, details, source, username) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
          ['DELETE', `Eliminata anagrafica prodotto: ${product.name}`, 'Gestionale', request.user.username]
        );
      }
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Impossibile eliminare, il prodotto è usato in alcune giacenze?' });
    }
  });
}

module.exports = productRoutes;

const db = require('../config/db');

async function settingsRoutes(fastify, options) {
  fastify.addHook('onRequest', fastify.authenticate);

  fastify.get('/', async (request, reply) => {
    try {
      const [rows] = await db.query('SELECT setting_key, setting_value FROM settings');
      const settingsObj = {};
      rows.forEach(r => {
        settingsObj[r.setting_key] = r.setting_value;
      });
      return settingsObj;
    } catch (err) {
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore recupero impostazioni' });
    }
  });

  fastify.post('/', async (request, reply) => {
    const settings = request.body; // Object of key-value pairs
    const conn = await db.getConnection();
    try {
      await conn.beginTransaction();
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(settings)) {
        await conn.query(
          'INSERT INTO settings (setting_key, setting_value) VALUES (?, ?) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE setting_value = ?',
          [key, value, value]
        );
      }
      await conn.commit();
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      await conn.rollback();
      fastify.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'Errore salvataggio impostazioni' });
    } finally {
      conn.release();
    }
  });
}
