                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const cur = parseFloat(qtyInput) || 0;
                      if (cur > 1) setQtyInput((cur - 1).toString());
                    }}
                    className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800 active:bg-slate-700 text-brand-white flex items-center justify-center font-black"
                  >
                    <Minus size={24} />
                  </button>
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      onWheel={(e) => e.target.blur()}
                      className="bg-slate-950 border-2 border-brand-blue text-brand-white text-center text-3xl font-black w-28 h-14 rounded-2xl focus:outline-none"
                      value={qtyInput}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        setQtyInput(e.target.value);
                        setError(null);
                      }}
                    />
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-bold mt-1">
                      {qtyInfo.hasBoxConversion
                        ? `${qtyInfo.primaryText} (${qtyInfo.qty} ${currentItem?.uom})`
                        : currentItem?.uom || 'Pezzi'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const cur = parseFloat(qtyInput) || 0;
                      setQtyInput((cur + 1).toString());
                    }}
                    className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800 active:bg-slate-700 text-brand-white flex items-center justify-center font-black"
                  >
                    <Plus size={24} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                onClick={handleConfirmQty}
                disabled={isProcessing || isConfirmLocked}
                className={`w-full font-black text-xl py-4 rounded-2xl transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg ${
                  isConfirmLocked || isProcessing
                    ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-500'
                    : 'bg-emerald-500 active:bg-emerald-400 text-emerald-950 active:scale-95 shadow-emerald-900/30'
                }`}
              >
                <Check size={24} strokeWidth={3} />
                <span>
                  {isProcessing
                    ? 'Salvataggio...'
                    : isConfirmLocked
                    ? 'Verifica Quantità...'
                    : `CONFERMA ${qtyInfo.primaryText}`}
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ====================================================================
          MODAL PERCORSO ORDINATO (Lista Tappe secondo Regole di Prelievo)
         ==================================================================== */}
      {showRouteModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex flex-col justify-end p-3">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-700 rounded-3xl max-h-[82vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-brand-white flex items-center gap-2">
                  <ListOrdered size={20} className="text-brand-blue" />
                  Percorso di Prelievo Ordinato
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                  Ordinato secondo le Regole di Prelievo (Tocca una riga per selezionarla)
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowRouteModal(false)}
                className="p-2 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl text-slate-300"
              >
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-2">
              {allOrderItems.map((it, idx) => {
                const isPicked = it.status === 'PICKED';
                const isCurrent = currentItem && it.id === currentItem.id && !isPicked;
                const itQtyInfo = getPickQuantityInfo(it);

                return (
                  <button
                    key={it.id}
                    disabled={isPicked}
                    onClick={() => {
                      const targetIndexInRemaining = items.findIndex((rem) => rem.id === it.id);
                      if (targetIndexInRemaining >= 0) {
                        setCurrentItemIndex(targetIndexInRemaining);
                        setError(null);
                        checkAndSkipPin(items[targetIndexInRemaining]);
                        setShowRouteModal(false);
                      }
                    }}
                    className={`w-full text-left p-3 rounded-2xl border-2 transition-all flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
                      isPicked
                        ? 'bg-emerald-950/20 border-emerald-500/30 opacity-60'
                        : isCurrent
                        ? 'bg-brand-blue/15 border-brand-blue'
                        : 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 active:border-slate-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
                        <span className="text-xs font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-800 text-brand-blue font-mono">
                          #{idx + 1}
                        </span>
                        <span className="font-black text-sm text-sky-400 truncate">
                          {formatLocationLabel(it)}
                        </span>
                        {isPicked && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                            ✓ Fatto
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {isCurrent && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase bg-brand-blue text-brand-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                            In Corso
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-brand-white font-bold text-sm truncate">{it.product_name}</p>
                      {it.batch && <p className="text-amber-400 text-xs font-mono">Lotto: {it.batch}</p>}
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                      <p className="text-emerald-400 font-black text-sm">{itQtyInfo.primaryText}</p>
                      {itQtyInfo.hasBoxConversion && (
                        <p className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] font-bold">({itQtyInfo.qty} pz)</p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default OutboundZebra;
=== FILE: frontend/src/zebra/Stow.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';
import { Scan, Box, MapPin, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, ArrowLeft, Search, Keyboard } from 'lucide-react';
import useBarcodeScanner from '../hooks/useBarcodeScanner';

const Stow = () => {
  const [pallet, setPallet] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('WAITING_PALLET'); 
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  
  const [pinInput, setPinInput] = useState('');
  
  const [locations, setLocations] = useState([]);
  const [zones, setZones] = useState([]);
  const [selectedZone, setSelectedZone] = useState('');
  const [selectedCol, setSelectedCol] = useState('');
  const [targetLocation, setTargetLocation] = useState(null);
  const [occupyingPallet, setOccupyingPallet] = useState(null);

  const [zoneSearch, setZoneSearch] = useState('');
  const [manualScanInput, setManualScanInput] = useState('');
  const [keyboardOpen, setKeyboardOpen] = useState(false);
  const scanDebounceRef = useRef(null);
  
  const [requirePins, setRequirePins] = useState(true);

  const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('maglite_token');

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchInit = async () => {
      try {
        const [resLoc, resSettings] = await Promise.all([
          axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/locations`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` } }),
          axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/settings`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` } })
        ]);
        setLocations(resLoc.data);
        const uniqueZones = [...new Set(resLoc.data.map(l => l.zone).filter(Boolean))].sort();
        setZones(uniqueZones);
        
        if (resSettings.data.require_location_pins) {
          setRequirePins(resSettings.data.require_location_pins === 'true');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Errore fetch", err);
      }
    };
    fetchInit();
  }, []);

  const handleScan = async (barcodeStr) => {
    const barcode = barcodeStr.trim();
    if (!barcode) return;
    
    if (status === 'WAITING_PALLET' || status === 'SUCCESS' || status === 'ERROR') {
      if (!barcode.startsWith('PAL-')) {
        setStatus('ERROR');
        setMessage('Scansiona un codice Paletta valido (PAL-...)');
        setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 3000);
        return;
      }
      
      try {
        const res = await axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/${barcode}`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` }
        });
        
        if (res.data.status !== 'PENDING' && res.data.status !== 'STOCKED') {
          setStatus('ERROR');
          setMessage(`Paletta in stato non valido: ${res.data.status}`);
          setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 4000);
          return;
        }

        setPallet(res.data);
        setPinInput('');
        setStatus('SELECT_ZONE');
      } catch (err) {
        setStatus('ERROR');
        setMessage(err.response?.data?.error || 'Paletta non trovata');
        setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 3000);
      }
    }
  };

  const inputRef = useRef(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    setKeyboardOpen(false);
    if (status === 'WAITING_PALLET' && inputRef.current) {
      setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }), 50);
    }
  }, [status]);

  useBarcodeScanner((barcode) => {
    if (status === 'WAITING_PALLET' || status === 'SUCCESS' || status === 'ERROR') {
      handleScan(barcode);
    }
  });

  const handleNumpad = async (num) => {
    if (pinInput.length < 3) {
      const newVal = pinInput + num;
      setPinInput(newVal);
      if (newVal.length === 3) {
        await processPin(newVal);
      }
    }
  };

  const deleteNumpad = () => setPinInput(prev => prev.slice(0, -1));

  const processPin = async (pin) => {
    const loc = locations.find(l => l.zone === selectedZone && l.pin === pin);
    if (!loc) {
      setStatus('ERROR');
      setMessage(`PIN ${pin} non trovato in ${selectedZone}`);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setPinInput('');
        setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
      }, 3000);
      return;
    }
    
    await checkOccupiedAndStow(loc.barcode, pin);
  };

  const checkOccupiedAndStow = async (locBarcode, pin = '') => {
    setTargetLocation(locBarcode);
    const loc = locations.find(l => l.barcode === locBarcode);
    if (loc && loc.pallet_code) {
      const existingPallets = loc.pallet_code.split(',').map(p => p.trim());
      if (existingPallets.length > 0 && !existingPallets.includes(pallet.pallet_code)) {
        setOccupyingPallet(existingPallets[0]);
        setStatus('WARNING_OCCUPIED');
        return;
      }
    }
    await executeStow(locBarcode, pin);
  };

  
  const executeMerge = async () => {
    try {
      await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/merge`, {
        source_code: pallet.pallet_code,
        target_code: occupyingPallet
      }, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` }
      });
      
      await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/stow`, {
        pallet_code: occupyingPallet,
        location: targetLocation,
        pin: pinInput,
        source: 'Zebra',
        force: true
      }, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` }
      });

      setStatus('SUCCESS');
      setMessage(`Unita e Spostata in: ${targetLocation}`);
      setPallet(null);
      setPinInput('');
      setTargetLocation(null);
      setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus('ERROR');
      setMessage(err.response?.data?.error || 'Errore durante frammentazione');
      setTimeout(() => {
        setPinInput('');
        if (requirePins) setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
        else setStatus('SELECT_ZONE');
      }, 3000);
    }
  };

  const executeStow = async (locationCode, pin, force = false) => {
    try {
      await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/stow`, {
        pallet_code: pallet.pallet_code,
        location: locationCode,
        pin: pin,
        source: 'Zebra',
        force
      }, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` }
      });

      setStatus('SUCCESS');
      setMessage(`Stivato in: ${locationCode}`);
      setPallet(null);
      setPinInput('');
      setTargetLocation(null);
      setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.response?.status === 409) {
        setOccupyingPallet(err.response.data.occupying_pallet);
        setStatus('WARNING_OCCUPIED');
      } else {
        setStatus('ERROR');
        setMessage(err.response?.data?.error || 'Posizione errata o occupata');
        setTimeout(() => {
          setPinInput('');
          if (requirePins) setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
          else setStatus('SELECT_ZONE');
        }, 3000);
      }
    }
  };

  const colsInZone = [...new Set(locations.filter(l => l.zone === selectedZone).map(l => l.col))].sort();
  const posInCol = [...new Set(locations.filter(l => l.zone === selectedZone && l.col === selectedCol).map(l => l.pos))].sort();

  return (
    <div className={`flex-1 p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center transition-colors duration-300 
      ${status === 'SUCCESS' ? 'bg-green-500/20' : status === 'ERROR' ? 'bg-rose-500/20' : 'bg-brand-black'}`}>
      
      {status === 'WAITING_PALLET' && (
        <div className="animate-fade-in-up w-full flex flex-col items-center">
          <div className="relative mb-6 flex justify-center w-full">
            <Scan size={84} className="text-brand-blue opacity-80" />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-blue opacity-20 blur-2xl rounded-full animate-pulse"></div>
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-brand-white mb-2 uppercase">Stivaggio</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-400 mb-6 max-w-[280px] text-base leading-relaxed mx-auto">
            Spara il codice a barre della <b>Paletta</b> che stai trasportando.
          </p>
          
          <div className="w-full max-w-xs bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-3.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-brand-blue">Laser Pronto</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  const next = !keyboardOpen;
                  setKeyboardOpen(next);
                  setTimeout(() => next && inputRef.current?.focus(), 40);
                }}
                className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                  keyboardOpen ? 'bg-brand-blue text-brand-black' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border border-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                <Keyboard size={14} />
                {keyboardOpen ? 'Chiudi Tastiera' : 'Tastiera'}
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                ref={inputRef}
                type="text"
                inputMode={keyboardOpen ? 'text' : 'none'}
                placeholder={keyboardOpen ? "Scrivi PAL-..." : "Attendo scansione..."}
                className="bg-slate-950 border-2 border-slate-700 p-3.5 rounded-xl text-base font-mono font-bold uppercase focus:border-brand-blue focus:outline-none text-brand-white w-full"
                value={manualScanInput}
                onBlur={() => setKeyboardOpen(false)}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const val = e.target.value.toUpperCase();
                  setManualScanInput(val);
                  if (scanDebounceRef.current) clearTimeout(scanDebounceRef.current);
                  const trimmed = val.trim();
                  if (!keyboardOpen && trimmed.startsWith('PAL-') && trimmed.length >= 12) {
                    scanDebounceRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
                      setManualScanInput('');
                      handleScan(trimmed);
                    }, 220);
                  }
                }}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' && manualScanInput.trim() !== '') {
                    if (scanDebounceRef.current) clearTimeout(scanDebounceRef.current);
                    const toScan = manualScanInput.trim();
                    setManualScanInput('');
                    handleScan(toScan);
                  }
                }}
              />
              <button 
                onClick={() => {
                  if (manualScanInput.trim() !== '') {
                    if (scanDebounceRef.current) clearTimeout(scanDebounceRef.current);
                    const toScan = manualScanInput.trim();
                    setManualScanInput('');
                    handleScan(toScan);
                  }
                }}
                className="bg-brand-blue hover:bg-sky-400 text-brand-black font-bold px-4 rounded-xl transition-colors shrink-0"
              >
                Vai
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'SELECT_ZONE' && pallet && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up text-left flex flex-col h-full py-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border-2 border-brand-blue rounded-3xl p-4 mb-4 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(14,165,233,0.2)]">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
              <span className="font-mono text-brand-blue font-bold text-xs bg-brand-blue/10 px-2 py-1 rounded">{pallet.pallet_code}</span>
              <span className="text-emerald-400 font-black text-base">
                {(() => {
                  const q = parseFloat(pallet.quantity) || 0;
                  const upb = parseInt(pallet.units_per_box, 10) || 1;
                  if (upb > 1 && q >= upb) {
                    const b = Math.floor(q / upb);
                    const rem = q % upb;
                    return `${b} SCAT.${rem > 0 ? ` + ${rem}` : ''} (${q} pz)`;
                  }
                  return `${q || '-'} ${pallet.uom || ''}`;
                })()}
              </span>
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-white text-left truncate">{pallet.product_name}</h3>
            {pallet.batch && <p className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-400 mt-0.5">Lotto: {pallet.batch}</p>}
          </div>
          
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-white mb-2">In quale ZONA?</h3>
          
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
            <input 
              type="text"
              placeholder={requirePins ? "Cerca zona..." : "O scrivi posizione per intero..."}
              className="w-full bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-700 p-3.5 rounded-xl text-base font-bold uppercase focus:border-brand-blue focus:ring-0 text-brand-white"
              value={zoneSearch}
              onChange={(e) => setZoneSearch(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                 if (e.key === 'Enter' && zoneSearch.trim() !== '') {
                    if (!requirePins && zoneSearch.includes('-')) {
                      checkOccupiedAndStow(zoneSearch.trim());
                    }
                 }
              }}
            />
            {!requirePins && (
              <button 
                onClick={() => {
                  if (zoneSearch.trim() !== '') checkOccupiedAndStow(zoneSearch.trim());
                }}
                className="bg-brand-blue hover:bg-sky-400 text-brand-black font-bold px-4 rounded-xl transition-colors shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center"
              >
                <CheckCircle2 size={24} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-3 pb-4">
            {zones.filter(z => z.toUpperCase().includes(zoneSearch)).map(z => (
              <button 
                key={z} 
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedZone(z);
                  if (requirePins) {
                    setPinInput('');
                    setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
                  } else {
                    setStatus('SELECT_COL');
                  }
                }}
                className="w-full py-4 px-6 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-xl font-bold text-brand-white text-left hover:border-brand-blue active:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
              >
                Zona: <span className="text-brand-blue ml-2">{z}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'SELECT_COL' && pallet && !requirePins && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up text-left flex flex-col h-full py-4">
          <button onClick={() => setStatus('SELECT_ZONE')} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-400 mb-4 active:text-brand-white">
            <ArrowLeft size={20} /> Cambia Zona
          </button>
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-white mb-2">Zona: {selectedZone}</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-400 mb-4">Scegli la Colonna (Lettera)</p>
          
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pb-4">
            {colsInZone.map(c => (
              <button 
                key={c} 
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedCol(c);
                  setStatus('SELECT_POS');
                }}
                className="py-4 px-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-2xl font-bold text-brand-blue text-center hover:border-brand-blue active:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
              >
                {c}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'SELECT_POS' && pallet && !requirePins && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up text-left flex flex-col h-full py-4">
          <button onClick={() => setStatus('SELECT_COL')} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-400 mb-4 active:text-brand-white">
            <ArrowLeft size={20} /> Cambia Colonna
          </button>
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-white mb-2">{selectedZone}-{selectedCol}</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-400 mb-4">Scegli il Livello (Numero)</p>
          
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 pb-4">
            {posInCol.map(p => (
              <button 
                key={p} 
                onClick={() => checkOccupiedAndStow(`${selectedZone}-${selectedCol}-${p}`)}
                className="py-4 px-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-400 text-center hover:border-emerald-400 active:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
              >
                {p}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'WAITING_PIN' && pallet && requirePins && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up flex flex-col h-full py-4">
          <button onClick={() => setStatus('SELECT_ZONE')} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-400 mb-4 active:text-brand-white">
            <ArrowLeft size={20} /> Cambia Zona
          </button>
          
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
            <p className="text-brand-blue font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-sm mb-1">Zona Selezionata</p>
            <p className="text-5xl font-black text-brand-white mb-6">{selectedZone}</p>
            
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mb-4">Leggi e digita il PIN esatto dello scaffale:</p>
            
            <div className="w-full flex gap-2 justify-center mb-6">
              {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`w-14 h-16 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-3xl font-bold border-2 ${i < pinInput.length ? 'border-brand-blue text-brand-white bg-slate-800' : 'border-slate-800 text-slate-600 bg-slate-950'}`}>
                  {pinInput[i] || ''}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 w-full mb-6">
              {[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9].map(num => (
                <button key={num} onClick={() => handleNumpad(num.toString())} className="h-14 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl text-brand-white text-2xl font-bold active:bg-slate-700 active:scale-95 transition-all">{num}</button>
              ))}
              <div className="h-14"></div>
              <button onClick={() => handleNumpad('0')} className="h-14 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl text-brand-white text-2xl font-bold active:bg-slate-700 active:scale-95 transition-all">0</button>
              <button onClick={deleteNumpad} className="h-14 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-500 rounded-xl font-bold active:bg-rose-500/20 active:scale-95 transition-all flex items-center justify-center">Canc</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'WARNING_OCCUPIED' && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up flex flex-col h-full py-4 items-center justify-center text-center">
          <div className="animate-[shake_0.5s_ease-in-out]">
            <AlertCircle size={80} className="text-rose-500 mb-6 mx-auto drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(244,63,94,0.5)]" />
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-brand-white mb-2">Posizione Occupata!</h2>
            <p className="text-rose-300 text-lg font-bold px-4 mb-6">
              La posizione <span className="text-white bg-rose-900/50 px-2 py-1 rounded mx-1">{targetLocation}</span> è già occupata dalla paletta <span className="text-white font-mono bg-amber-900/80 px-2 py-1 rounded mx-1">{occupyingPallet}</span>.
            </p>
          </div>
          
          <button 
            onClick={executeMerge}
            className="w-full py-4 bg-amber-500 text-amber-950 text-xl font-black rounded-2xl active:bg-amber-400 mb-3 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(245,158,11,0.3)]"
          >
            UNISCI / FRAMMENTA
          </button>
          
          <button 
            onClick={() => executeStow(targetLocation, pinInput, true)}
            className="w-full py-4 bg-rose-600 text-white text-xl font-black rounded-2xl active:bg-rose-500 mb-3"
          >
            FORZA COMUNQUE
          </button>
          
          <button 
            onClick={() => {
              setPinInput('');
              if (requirePins) setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
              else setStatus('SELECT_ZONE');
            }}
            className="w-full py-4 bg-slate-800 text-brand-white text-xl font-bold rounded-2xl active:bg-slate-700"
          >
            Annulla (Cambia)
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'SUCCESS' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center animate-[bounce_0.5s_ease-in-out]">
          <CheckCircle2 size={100} className="text-green-400 mb-6 drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(74,222,128,0.5)]" />
          <h2 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-white mb-4">Ottimo!</h2>
          <p className="text-green-300 text-xl font-bold">{message}</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'ERROR' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center animate-[shake_0.5s_ease-in-out]">
          <AlertCircle size={100} className="text-rose-500 mb-6 drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(244,63,94,0.5)]" />
          <h2 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-white mb-4">Attenzione</h2>
          <p className="text-rose-300 text-xl font-bold px-4">{message}</p>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default Stow;
=== FILE: frontend/src/zebra/ZebraApp.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Routes, Route, useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PackagePlus, ArrowRightLeft, PackageMinus, LogOut, Scan, Download } from 'lucide-react';
import useBarcodeScanner from '../hooks/useBarcodeScanner';
import Stow from './Stow';
import InboundZebra from './InboundZebra';

const TouchButton = ({ icon: Icon, label, onClick }) => (
  <button 
    onClick={onClick}
    className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 rounded-3xl border-2 border-slate-800 shadow-xl transition-all active:scale-95 bg-slate-900 text-brand-white hover:border-brand-blue"
  >
    <Icon size={56} className="mb-4 text-brand-blue" />
    <span className="text-xl font-bold tracking-wide">{label}</span>
  </button>
);

const TopBar = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const isHome = location.pathname === '/zebra';

  return (
    <header className="bg-brand-black border-b border-slate-800 p-5 flex justify-between items-center sticky top-0 z-10 shadow-lg">
      <div 
        className={`flex items-center gap-4 ${!isHome ? 'cursor-pointer active:scale-95 transition-transform' : ''}`} 
        onClick={() => !isHome && navigate('/zebra')}
      >
        <div className="w-12 h-12 flex items-center justify-center">
          <img src="/logo.png" alt="MagLite Logo" className="max-w-full max-h-full object-contain drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(169,218,255,0.4)]" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-brand-white font-bold text-xl leading-tight tracking-tight">MagLite</h1>
          <p className="text-brand-blue text-[11px] uppercase font-bold tracking-[0.2em]">Operatore</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <button 
        onClick={() => {
          localStorage.removeItem('maglite_token');
          localStorage.removeItem('maglite_user');
          window.location.href = '/login';
        }}
        className="p-4 rounded-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-brand-white active:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
      >
        <LogOut size={24} />
      </button>
    </header>
  );
};

const Dashboard = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 p-6 flex flex-col gap-6 overflow-y-auto w-full max-w-md mx-auto">
      <div className="mb-2 mt-4">
        <h2 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-white tracking-tight">Scanner</h2>
        <p className="text-brand-blue text-base font-medium mt-1">Seleziona l'azione da eseguire</p>
      </div>
      
      
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5">
          <TouchButton 
            icon={Download} 
            label="SPUNTA ENTRATA" 
            onClick={() => navigate('/zebra/inbound')} 
          />
        <TouchButton 
          icon={PackagePlus} 
          label="STIVAGGIO (Messa a dimora)" 
          onClick={() => navigate('/zebra/stow')} 
        />
        <TouchButton 
          icon={ArrowRightLeft} 
          label="HANDLING (Spostamento)" 
          onClick={() => navigate('/zebra/handling')} 
        />
        <TouchButton 
          icon={PackageMinus} 
          label="PICKING (Prelievo)" 
          onClick={() => navigate('/zebra/outbound')} 
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const ZebraOperationPage = ({ title }) => {
  const [lastScan, setLastScan] = useState(null);
  const [flash, setFlash] = useState(false);

  useBarcodeScanner((barcode) => {
    setLastScan(barcode);
    setFlash(true);
    setTimeout(() => setFlash(false), 300);
  });

  return (
    <div className={`flex-1 p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center transition-colors duration-300 ${flash ? 'bg-brand-blue/20' : 'bg-brand-black'}`}>
      <div className="relative mb-8">
        <Scan size={96} className="text-brand-blue opacity-80" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-blue opacity-20 blur-2xl rounded-full animate-pulse"></div>
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-white mb-4 uppercase">{title}</h2>
      <p className="text-slate-400 mb-10 max-w-[280px] text-lg leading-relaxed">
        Punta il laser del terminale Zebra e premi il grilletto per scansionare.
      </p>
      {lastScan ? (
        <div className="bg-brand-blue text-brand-black px-8 py-5 rounded-3xl shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(14,165,233,0.4)] animate-[bounce_0.5s_ease-in-out]">
          <p className="text-sm uppercase font-extrabold tracking-widest mb-1 opacity-80">Codice Rilevato</p>
          <p className="text-4xl font-black">{lastScan}</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="border-2 border-slate-800 border-dashed rounded-3xl px-8 py-6 text-slate-500 font-semibold text-lg">
          In attesa di scansione...
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import OutboundZebra from './OutboundZebra';
import Handling from './Handling';

const ZebraApp = () => {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-black flex flex-col font-sans text-brand-white antialiased selection:bg-brand-blue selection:text-brand-black">
      <TopBar />
      
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/inbound" element={<InboundZebra />} />
        <Route path="/" element={<Dashboard />} />
        <Route path="/stow" element={<Stow />} />
        <Route path="/outbound" element={<OutboundZebra />} />
        <Route path="/handling" element={<Handling />} />
        <Route path="/out-stock" element={<ZebraOperationPage title="Picking" />} />
      </Routes>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ZebraApp;
=== FILE: database/init.sql ===
CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS maglite_db;
USE maglite_db;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS USERS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    role ENUM('developer', 'backoffice', 'operator') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'operator',
    requires_password_change BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    preferences JSON DEFAULT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS CUSTOMERS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    unique_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    business_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    vat_number VARCHAR(50),
    address TEXT,
    phone VARCHAR(50),
    email VARCHAR(100)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS PRODUCTS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    sku VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    uom VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Scatole',
    units_per_box INT DEFAULT 1,
    boxes_per_pallet INT DEFAULT 1,
    customer_id INT NOT NULL,
    notes TEXT,
    default_article_number VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL,
    flags JSON DEFAULT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES CUSTOMERS(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS LOCATIONS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    zone VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    col VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    pos VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    barcode VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    pin VARCHAR(5) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS FREE_LOCATIONS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS PALLETS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    pallet_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    customer_id INT NOT NULL,
    product_id INT NOT NULL,
    quantity DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    units_per_box INT DEFAULT 1,
    pallet_uom VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT NULL,
    batch VARCHAR(100),
    warehouse VARCHAR(50),
    status ENUM('PENDING', 'STOCKED', 'SHIPPED') DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    inbound_ddt_id INT DEFAULT NULL,
    location VARCHAR(50),
    notes TEXT,
    client_pallet_number VARCHAR(100),
    client_article_number VARCHAR(100),
    expiration_date DATE,
    shipped_at DATETIME DEFAULT NULL,
    flags JSON DEFAULT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES CUSTOMERS(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES PRODUCTS(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS OUTBOUND_ORDERS (
  id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  order_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  customer_id INT NOT NULL,
  exit_date DATE NOT NULL,
  status ENUM('PENDING', 'PICKING', 'READY', 'SHIPPED') DEFAULT 'PENDING',
  client_ddt VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  picking_operator VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  start_picking_at DATETIME DEFAULT NULL,
  end_picking_at DATETIME DEFAULT NULL,
  shipped_at DATETIME DEFAULT NULL,
  created_by VARCHAR(50),
  created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES CUSTOMERS(id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS OUTBOUND_ITEMS (
  id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id INT NOT NULL,
  pallet_id INT DEFAULT NULL,
  pallet_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  product_id INT NOT NULL,
  batch VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  quantity_required DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
  quantity_picked DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
  requested_uom VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  status ENUM('PENDING', 'PICKED') DEFAULT 'PENDING',
  picked_at DATETIME DEFAULT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES OUTBOUND_ORDERS(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES PRODUCTS(id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS AUDIT_LOGS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    action VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    details TEXT NOT NULL,
    source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    username VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'Sistema',
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS billing_rules (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    rule_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    description VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    default_price DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
    rule_type ENUM('EVENT', 'STORAGE', 'FLAG', 'DISCOUNT_PERCENT', 'DISCOUNT_FIXED', 'MANUAL') NOT NULL
);

INSERT IGNORE INTO billing_rules (rule_code, description, default_price, rule_type) VALUES 
('IN_PALLET', 'Ingresso Paletta', 20.00, 'EVENT'),
('OUT_PALLET', 'Uscita Paletta', 20.00, 'EVENT'),
('SOSTA_15', 'Sosta (Ingresso <= 15)', 4.00, 'STORAGE'),
('SOSTA_MAGGIORE_15', 'Sosta (Ingresso > 15)', 8.00, 'STORAGE');

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS client_tariffs (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    customer_id INT NOT NULL,
    rule_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    custom_price DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE KEY(customer_id, rule_code)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (
    setting_key VARCHAR(100) PRIMARY KEY,
    setting_value TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS invoices (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    customer_id INT NOT NULL,
    period_month INT NOT NULL,
    period_year INT NOT NULL,
    base_total DECIMAL(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    vat_amount DECIMAL(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    grand_total DECIMAL(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    invoice_data JSON DEFAULT NULL,
    is_paid BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE KEY unique_proforma (customer_id, period_month, period_year)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS expenses (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    description VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    amount DECIMAL(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    expense_date DATE NOT NULL,
    category VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Generale',
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS INBOUND_DDTS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    ddt_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    customer_id INT NOT NULL,
    status ENUM('PENDING', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED', 'DISCREPANCY') DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    closed_at DATETIME DEFAULT NULL,
    notes TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES CUSTOMERS(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS INBOUND_DDT_ITEMS (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    inbound_ddt_id INT NOT NULL,
    product_id INT NOT NULL,
    sku VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL,
    product_name VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT NULL,
    product_uom VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT NULL,
    expected_quantity DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    actual_quantity DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    units_per_box INT,
    boxes_per_pallet INT,
    pallet_uom VARCHAR(50),
    batch VARCHAR(100),
    client_pallet_number VARCHAR(100),
    client_article_number VARCHAR(100),
    expiration_date DATE,
    location VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT NULL,
    notes TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (inbound_ddt_id) REFERENCES INBOUND_DDTS(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES PRODUCTS(id)
);

-- TMS TABLES
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS TMS_DRIVERS (
    id VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    vehicle VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    sort_order INT DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS TMS_TRIPS (
    id VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
    driver_id VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT NULL,
    customer VARCHAR(255),
    ref VARCHAR(100),
    pickup VARCHAR(255),
    pickupTime DATETIME,
    delivery VARCHAR(255),
    deliveryTime DATETIME,
    revenue DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    cost DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'UNASSIGNED',
    title VARCHAR(255),
    notes TEXT,
    sort_order INT DEFAULT 0
);
